# DN — Change Log

### CHG-514 | Dashboard date switches and Activity context reading | 13 November 2026 | OldVersion development

- **Dashboard:** Improve Upcoming/On This Day date-view label visibility (13px on phones), Hide/Show caption floor (12.5px), and show focus when keyboard users enter nested Moment/Activity scroll regions. Keep the 44px date toggles, Dashboard Task-first/Moment-second/Activity-third order, default-collapsed Tasks, nested scrolling and all existing view-state business logic unchanged. No PDF/Story edit.
- **Activities:** Increase photo-led card Moment and date context to 13px on phones in both main Activities and Moment-local cards. Preserve title/status first row, two-line parent clamp, image originals, record links, swipe actions, filters, sorting, records and layout.
- **Verification:** Extend mobile Home and Activity cards assertions; add `[verify-dashboard-activities]` opt-in browser lane running four existing affected specs. CI app smoke/build/docs and approved ExpenseTracker development deployment only. Native Safari and VoiceOver require manual acceptance.
- **Scope:** `OldVersion` only. No production, main, Lifehub, code-list, schema, Guest tour/version, backend or PDF change.

### CHG-513 | Preserve compact desktop transaction disclosure in new browser acceptance | 13 November 2026 | OldVersion development

- CHG-512 exact-SHA Desktop Shell browser run `43344688741` again passed 35/36 tests. The remaining new desktop case checked the phone-only 44px disclosure hit-area; the established desktop disclosure is 28px high and was never changed by CHG-511. All iPhone visual and interaction checks passed.
- Keep the existing desktop density rather than quietly changing card heights. Require 44px phone touch disclosure, 28px existing desktop disclosure, 13px desktop expanded content, full original notes, and no source/overflow mutations. This changes only the scoped synthetic acceptance assertion, not app UI or business logic.
- Repeat `[verify-desktop-shell]`, documentation smoke and automatic development deployment on exact follow-up SHA. No main/prod push, app version, Guest tour or backend changes.

### CHG-512 | Finance reading test preserves intentional desktop metadata scale | 13 November 2026 | OldVersion development

- CHG-511 targeted Desktop Shell run `43344422896` passed 35/36 browser tests; only the new 1280px Transaction metadata check expected 13px despite the spec intentionally preserving the previous desktop 12.5px caption style. Phone 320/390px, expanded detail, Task/Finance actions, desktop shell and remaining cases all passed.
- Correct only the assertion: require 13px for phone metadata; allow the unchanged 12.5px semantic caption size for desktop. Keep 13px minimum for expanded Details at every tested size, existing scroll, storage and interaction assertions. No CSS, component, backend, Guest data, version or production change.
- Repeat the existing opt-in `[verify-desktop-shell]` suite against the exact new SHA and confirm documentation, app smoke and development deployment. Actual Safari/VoiceOver remain manual gates.

### CHG-510 | Keyboard modality for PDF focus acceptance | 12 November 2026 | OldVersion development

- The CHG-509 PDF suite on commit `24f0fe4` passed its 13 existing cases but the two newly extended accessibility cases expected a `:focus-visible` ring after programmatic focus while Chromium remained in pointer modality. Browser behavior is intentional: mouse focus does not show keyboard-only outlines.
- Correct the test by sending an actual Tab keyboard input before focusing the page button. Keep the real computed-outline assertion, all existing PDF page/zoom/reopen/navigation/storage checks, and production code unchanged. Do not replace `:focus-visible` with an always-on outline.
- Retest the exact new SHA with the existing `[verify-230-iphone-pdf]` and `[verify-234]` opt-in gates. No user-data or export change, production deploy, app version, Guest fixture, Lifehub or main modification.

### CHG-509 | PDF navigation announcement, focus clarity and fullscreen reopen | 12 November 2026 | OldVersion development

- **Accessibility:** Existing normal and fullscreen PDF page navigation gain screen-reader-only, polite current-page announcements. The modal hides the underlying navigation from interaction while open; normal and fullscreen labels remain distinct. Explicit keyboard-visible focus rings on existing page navigation and thumbnails are scoped to PDF Studio only.
- **iPhone viewer:** Reopening fullscreen on the same PDF page now begins at top-left, rather than inheriting a previous scroll offset. Preserve the existing page-change reset, persistent zoom selection, pagination, active thumbnail, image bytes, export guards and original records.
- **Verification:** Extend synthetic 320px/dark and 390px/light PDF page-flow tests for status, keyboard focus, page change, full-screen reopen and unchanged Guest data. Run the existing `[verify-230-iphone-pdf]` and `[verify-234]` suites on exact SHA, plus build and documentation governance. No new Story changes require separate Guest sample refresh.

### CHG-508 | Correct new Story/PDF synthetic acceptance assumptions | 12 November 2026 | OldVersion development

- **Exact failure:** At CHG-507 commit `41b882e`, [PDF run 43238215896](https://github.com/Divin-kumar/DN_APP_MAIN/actions/runs/43238215896) passed 13 existing cases but the two new page-scroll cases stopped on a doubly escaped page-count regex, returning NaN before navigation checks. [Story run 43238215631](https://github.com/Divin-kumar/DN_APP_MAIN/actions/runs/43238215631) passed 27 existing cases but the two new portrait/landscape cases expected an <img> before the real original's IntersectionObserver lazy-loading region was reached.
- **Correction:** Parse the existing page-count label with decimal `parseInt` and scroll the existing preview button into view before asserting its original image has loaded. Keep the actual preview-scroll reset, full-screen navigation, rendered image geometry, original preview and record-preservation assertions unchanged. Do not bypass checks, alter timing or disable lazy loading.
- **Scope:** Two tests and the change log only. App source/styles, PDF bytes, data, Guest tour, backend, version, Firebase rules, production and main remain unchanged. Repeat the opt-in `[verify-234]` and `[verify-230-iphone-pdf]` jobs on the new SHA; physical iOS Safari/VoiceOver is still separate.

### CHG-505 | Reveal offscreen large form editor on phone keyboard focus | 12 November 2026 | OldVersion development

- **Issue:** Shared mobile dialogs already moved short focused fields above sticky actions, but skipped oversized text editors even when the entire editor was below the visible scroll area. A keyboard focus could therefore leave the beginning of a long draft inaccessible.
- **Change:** Use a small shared geometry helper to reveal only the top of a fully hidden oversized editor. Preserve the existing native caret scroll for partly visible writing, short-field handling, keyboard inset, header/backdrop animation and swipe dismissal.
- **Evidence:** Added deterministic viewport geometry tests and a synthetic keyboard browser case at 320/390px for Task notes, including cancelled-draft/Guest storage checks; opt-in [verify-230-form-ui] now runs Node geometry, lint, build and focused browser tests. Exact-SHA CI results must be checked after the commit, and real iPhone Safari remains unverified.
- **Scope:** OldVersion and standing Firebase development site only. No change to form content, saved data, authentication, filter/sort, Story/PDF, tour version, production DN site, main or Lifehub.

### CHG-504 | Wait for finished applied-filter reveal animation in acceptance | 12 November 2026 | OldVersion development

- **Exact failed check:** The independent filter/sort workflow at `d36a3c7`, [run 43202575914](https://github.com/Divin-kumar/DN_APP_MAIN/actions/runs/43202575914), passed **39 of 41 browser checks**, including existing list/Task sorting and filter editing, but the two new +N tests at 320 and 390px read chip coordinates during the intended smooth scroll. Their earlier `scrollLeft > 0` check confirms movement started, not arrival.
- **Correction:** Keep the strict third-chip visibility and first-chip/collapse assertions, but poll for full chip intersection with the visible rail before reading final coordinates. Do not remove the geometry check, change scroll duration, disable animation or weaken the saved-data/overflow checks.
- **Scope:** Test timing and audit only. Production code, user preferences, demo fixtures, accessibility behavior, Firebase configuration, version and release permissions remain unchanged. Re-run `[verify-filter-sort-ui]` on the new exact SHA; physical iPhone Safari remains unverified.

### CHG-503 | Applied filter +N rail continuity and keyboard access | 12 November 2026 | OldVersion development

- **Observed issue:** Expanding a clipped applied-filter chip rail always scrolled to its maximum right edge. With several conditions, the first newly disclosed rule could be skipped; collapsing could leave the initial chips offscreen on narrow phones.
- **Correction:** Scroll only the chip rail to the first newly disclosed rule, keep the existing +N/Less action fixed beside it, and reset that rail to its start on collapse. Permit Left/Right navigation when the rail itself has keyboard focus, without intercepting editing controls. Honor Reduce Motion.
- **Preserved:** The same lightweight single row, chip labels, drag/reorder, removal and inline editing, OR/AND logic, search, sort, counts, saved state, sample data, authentication, financial calculations and Story/PDF. No new features, account mutations or change to Guests' existing data. Existing tour instructions remain accurate, so no tour version bump.
- **Evidence:** Source-level anchored replacement checks plus new 320/390px Playwright assertions in `e2e/applied-filter-edit.spec.js` for +N/Less, first revealed chip, fixed overflow control, keyboard scroll, page overflow and no console errors. Exact-SHA CI results pending until workflow completion; physical iPhone Safari unverified.
- **Release:** OldVersion only, `[verify-filter-sort-ui]` opt-in nonproduction checks and standing automatic ExpenseTracker development preview. No `main` write, production deploy, version bump, Firebase rule or Lifehub change.

- **Diagnosis:** Targeted Chromium run 43130132420 passed Node, lint, build, 29 browser cases and documentation, but the new cross-route test failed nine Cancel snapshots by comparing storage before first-open list-preference migration finished. The tenth failure expected three Task sort columns at 844×390 even though the existing coarse-pointer short-landscape CSS legitimately renders two.

**Policy:** Record meaningful changes below this introduction, latest first by descending CHG date and sequence. Preserve historical entry text; record corrections in a new entry. A new entry is not required for every push or ordinary documentation maintenance. Earlier mandatory-per-push and append-at-bottom instructions inside historical records are superseded by the owner's 2026-10-22 direction. The [master](MASTER_SOURCE_OF_TRUTH.md) defines current behavior; [AGENTS.md](../AGENTS.md) defines maintenance rules.

**Historical coverage limitation:** Earlier repository history has not been fully reconstructed. Existing records are preserved evidence and may describe superseded behavior or policies.

## CHG-20261113-528 | Measure Activity Detail tap targets after route motion

- **Executed:** Full Chromium run `43444105469` on `f2f205f` passed 915 checks, including the two editor regressions, with 2 skipped and one Activity Detail geometry failure. A Chromium reproduction confirmed that fractional route translation can return a DOMRect height of 43.999969px for a 44px control.
- **Correction:** Recheck the real rendered bounds as the route settles while still requiring two controls, each at least 44px wide and high. The Activity Detail suite passed all 16 focused cases across four repeats. The full exact-source verification and production Hosting readback still apply.

## CHG-20261113-527 | Finish the shared card action exit before opening editors

- **Executed:** Full browser run `43437461050` on `c57ab14` finished with 914 passing, 2 skipped and 2 failing cases. Local Chromium reproduced both failures. The Dashboard and Transaction traces showed that the menu reached its exit transform while opacity and the queued Edit action stalled.
- **Correction:** Drive the shared menu fade and movement on the same Motion frame loop and keep the first action selected during exit. The Transaction regression opens More options before checking that the saved record's time field is blank. The public Guest tour already describes the same actions, so its version and fixtures stay in place.
- **Executed after correction:** 23 focused Chromium checks passed, including the two reproduced failures and the four-module menu animation check. All 626 Node tests, lint, the production build and the documentation check passed locally.
- **Release:** The owner approved production publication of v2.3.11 from OldVersion. The existing exact-source full pre-release workflow and Hosting readback still apply. Physical iPhone Safari remains unverified.

## CHG-20261113-526 | Verify action completion before reopening menus in release checks

- **Executed:** The v2.3.11 release candidate passed the About preflight. The full browser suite reported 911 passing, 2 skipped and 5 failing cases in run 43433597585.
- **Correction:** In Activity and Task tests, wait for the reopened status before choosing Edit because the mutation follows the action menu exit. For the Dashboard visibility tests, freeze only the calendar date so real animation frames and timers can run across page loads. Keep the existing persistence, visibility and draft assertions.
- **Release:** Repeat the affected journeys in parallel with the required full verification on the follow-up source. Production remains gated on a successful full run.

## CHG-20261113-525 | Prepare DN v2.3.11 for production and reset the development changelog

- **Release scope:** Package the approved development baseline `6fbdb21` as v2.3.11, with release notes covering the changes since v2.3.10. The owner authorized promotion to production and cleanup of About in both environments.
- **About:** Show the latest published release with earlier versions available under one collapsed disclosure. Development uses the production baseline for its release highlights and lists only commits ahead of it. The development Hosting workflow compares both the index and About ledger to the live files and checks the post-publication baseline.
- **Verification:** The existing independent full pre-release workflow must pass on the exact source SHA before the production workflow can publish. The production workflow then verifies the live index and triggers the development baseline refresh. The Guest tour does not depend on release history, so its version stays the same.

## CHG-20261113-524 | Improve keyboard access in searchable dropdowns

- **Search:** Home and End move the caret within the search field. Tab reaches Clear search, Shift+Tab returns to the field, and Clear supports native button activation. Escape returns focus to the dropdown trigger within Refine.
- **Text input:** Composition keystrokes stay with the input method so they can complete without selecting an option or dismissing the menu.
- **Executed:** All 23 focused Chromium checks passed across dropdown keyboard access, Task and Dashboard Refine, and simulated iPhone touch. Lint and build passed. Physical iPhone Safari and VoiceOver remain unverified.

## CHG-20261108-504 | Authorize guarded DN v2.3.10 production publication

**Authorization:** The owner explicitly requested production deployment of the subtle mobile bottom-sheet glass, compact frosted profile popover, and previously approved UI closing/gesture refinements, excluding the cancelled + long-press action wheel.

**Independent verification:** Exact code candidate `aeaf5f9176918b013b26ffa4fe72d8c10ddb35fb` passed **57/57** focused Chromium browser checks in [GitHub run 42435583454](https://github.com/Divin-kumar/DN_APP_MAIN/actions/runs/42435583454), including mobile sheet translucency at 320/390/430px and profile menu contrast/hit areas at all three widths in light and dark mode. Unit tests, lint, build, dependency audit and documentation governance also passed in [run 42435583447](https://github.com/Divin-kumar/DN_APP_MAIN/actions/runs/42435583447). No separate full pre-release E2E was requested for this minor patch.

**Release action:** Trigger the existing guarded OldVersion-only **fast patch** production workflow using `[deploy-live]` at this documentation-only commit. The workflow must verify v2.3.10 is greater than the live v2.3.9, rerun Node/lint/audit/build, deploy only Firebase site `dn-10` in project `expense-tracker-divin`, and compare live HTML bytes to the exact release build. The automatically deployed ExpenseTracker Development site and Lifehub remain separate; no backend, schema, rules or account data changes. This record is authorization and expectation, not a claim that publication has already succeeded.

## CHG-20261108-503 | Correct modal CSS precedence and profile option hit area

**Failed evidence:** [Targeted browser run 42435297483](https://github.com/Divin-kumar/DN_APP_MAIN/actions/runs/42435297483) at `d5d16c4` recorded **50 passes and seven failures**. Four profile tests detected option heights below the guaranteed 44px minimum during the menu entrance. Three glass tests measured fully opaque shared ModalDialog backgrounds because later legacy theme rules overrode the initial translucent declarations.

**Repair:** Restore 46px minimum profile options while keeping the reduced menu width, outer padding and glass surface. Make the shared mobile-sheet CSS selector explicitly match the modal viewport and `section[role=dialog]` with a higher priority than legacy modal backgrounds, preserving exact theme-specific glass opacity and child legibility. Retain the new assertions; do not weaken their opacity or touch-size checks. Rerun targeted browser verification before any production release.

**Scope:** Presentation-only follow-up on OldVersion; v2.3.10 candidate and data behavior unchanged. No Firebase production publication or other-app deployment authorized by this commit itself; owner approval remains subject to passing gates.

## CHG-20261108-502 | Subtle glass mobile sheets, compact glass profile and v2.3.10 release preparation

**Approval:** Owner requested a slightly glassy treatment on all bottom sheets and a compact lightly frosted top-right profile menu, cancelled the proposed long-press + action wheel, and explicitly authorized DN production publication.

**Implementation:** Apply 93% light/95% dark card surface opacity and 20px backdrop blur to mobile shared sheets only. Preserve opaque children, gesture-safe form fields and desktop dialogs. Narrow the profile popover from 274px to 248px with less internal whitespace; maintain 44px touch and keyboard targets, account identity, Profile/Insights/Settings/Logout. ## CHG-20261108-501 | Animate action dismissal and enlarge safe mobile sheet swipe area

**Owner observation:** Actions in Activities, Moments and related lists appeared to open smoothly but closed instantly, while bottom navigation/forms only responded to downward swipes from the very top. Give the independent Moment overflow menu an explicit Framer entrance/exit. In the shared mobile sheet, allow a 118px downward-intent swipe from noninteractive content at the top of its scroll body, while ignoring touch on fields/buttons/links/labels or inside nested scrollers. Increase its close hit target to 52px; close-button, backdrop and Escape now request reverse-slide dismissal and respect guarded onClose. No automatic closing when content is scrolled. Add regression checks for observable menu exit across Moments/Activities/Tasks/Transactions and gesture safety in Navigate & Create and forms.

**Rejected experimental idea (not implemented):** A long-press + drag-to-choice radial menu would conflict with the already-approved long-drag move-to-edge, single-tap menu and double-tap Back. Edge-constrained left/right mirrors would also be harder to discover and use; leave the current visible actions unchanged.

**Scope/verification:** Changes committed only to OldVersion, with the existing authorized ExpenseTracker development publication and opt-in independent browser verification; live iPhone Safari evidence remains to be collected. Bump public Guest tour to `2026.11.08.1` without touching personal Guest edits. No production `dn-10` approval, app-version change, `main`, Lifehub, Firebase backend/rules, authentication, financial data, Story/PDF or destructive action semantics.

## CHG-20261108-500 | Assert actual Safari keyboard dock geometry, not computed bottom text

**Focused evidence:** GitHub [run 42431630994](https://github.com/Divin-kumar/DN_APP_MAIN/actions/runs/42431630994) at `73b0adf` passed 43 of 44 mobile-command/browser cases, including the existing viewport-jitter test, but the newly added combined-height keyboard case incorrectly asserted `getComputedStyle(bar).bottom === 'auto'`. Chromium reports a resolved `12px` even when the new top anchor has placed the dock at its correct visual coordinate; the preceding assertion had already confirmed the correct 832px bottom after viewport pan.

**Focused test repair:** Keep the exact bottom-coordinate assertion, and require top=780px and dock height=52px after a synthetic Safari 330px pan. This checks the visible dock rather than a browser-specific computed-style string. No application CSS, keyboard behavior, authentication, finance, PDF, saved records, app version, Firebase configuration, `main` or production changes. Request the same independent mobile-command suite again; physical iPhone Safari still needs owner testing.

## CHG-20261108-499 | Keep iPhone Search above the keyboard through Safari viewport shrink and pan

**User report:** After the preceding mobile keyboard adjustment, tapping Search could still make the fixed dock disappear below the iOS keyboard. The earlier code assumed `window.innerHeight` would stay unchanged while `visualViewport.height` shrank, and it repeatedly forced `window.scrollY` to zero while iOS was trying to pan the focused input into view.

**Fix:** Capture pre-focus viewport height; detect a keyboard even when both layout and visual heights shrink or `offsetTop` moves. While keyboard-open, anchor the shared Search/Refine/+ dock to `visualViewport.offsetTop + visualViewport.height - 60px` instead of applying a bottom inset that can become zero. Suspend independently floating + during Search focus or keyboard visibility; restore its saved position afterwards. Do not fight Safari's own window scroll pan; keep the existing independent content-canvas scroll-restoration. Extend focused Playwright tests to model a simultaneous inner-height shrink and visual-viewport pan.

**Scope/validation:** OldVersion-only front-end layout change with independent opt-in mobile tests and automatic ExpenseTracker development preview. No change to data, finance logic, PDFs, authentication, Firebase configuration, other Hosting sites, `main`, production `dn-10`, Guest tour or app version. Synthetic Chromium viewport tests cannot fully replicate the iPhone software keyboard, so real Safari remains an acceptance condition. No production release requested or triggered.

## CHG-20261107-498 | Preserve immutable CHG-497 and clarify keyboard tap timing

**Audit correction:** The prior documentation-only follow-up changed the words of CHG-497 after its first commit, violating the permanent historical entry contract. Restore CHG-497 verbatim from first recorded commit `9b69d6f`; superseding details belong in this new entry, not an edit to an earlier entry.

**Actual final behavior:** In the mobile command dock, an activation of the universal + blurs a focused Search input **on click after pointerup**, not on pointerdown. This prevents the keyboard-driven dock resize from moving the + before a touch tap completes. The original 320ms tap-versus-double-tap logic is unchanged. The focused synthetic keyboard test also follows real visualViewport geometry: when the viewport has a 25px top offset, its visible bottom is 535px and the dock's bottom is 527px, 8px above that edge. This corrects the test's initially incorrect 502px assertion and adds the Search-to-+ focus handoff regression.

**Validation:** GitHub documentation audit previously reported edited history on CHG-497; restoration is submitted to run that immutable check again. This is a documentation-only correction at this commit. No further application code, version, account data, backend/rules, other Hosting site, `main` or production release changes.

## CHG-20261106-480 | Make the universal creator visible and animate real sheet movement

**Owner feedback:** The first 48px journaling illustration was barely visible on an iPhone; opening the action sheet and form appeared to blink, with no convincing entrance/exit. The 48px SVG used a very small subject, the command bar used an immediate `visibility:hidden`, and selecting Create mounted a second modal while the first sheet was exiting. Existing visibility-only tests could not detect this visual failure.

**Fix:** Enlarge the single illustrated button to 68px with a blue rounded-square background, redraw the character to fill the surface, and animate real SVG head/hand/float motion. Recompute drag snapping and safe clamping for the new dimensions, while retaining Search/Refine, the current five destinations, keyboard/VoiceOver labels, stored dock position, existing form handlers and Reduce Motion. Remove only the command bar's instant hide behavior, crossfade/move the character, and serialize command-sheet exit before form entrance. Animate command and universal-create sheets a visible 148px vertically on entry and a matching reverse closure; bypass competing legacy keyframe animations for those sheets alone.

**Compatibility:** Changes are presentational. No Firebase/Guest scope, persistent record, finance balance, backups, original attachments, Story/PDF, desktop navigation, or version number changes. The public Guest tour wording (v2026.11.06.3) still explains the illustrated creator, so an automatic reset would be inappropriate.

**Verification in progress:** Initial run [42233351121](https://github.com/Divin-kumar/DN_APP_MAIN/actions/runs/42233351121) passed lint/build and 25/38 browser cases, including the frame-sampled entrance/exit acceptance, but its 12 legacy assumptions required a 48px dock; one outdated SVG path assertion still referenced `.dn-mascot-pencil`. Documentation governance also exposed a source-contract test expecting the literal `quickCreateType === 'transaction'` expression. The render guard now uses that original source expression with `!commandHandoff`, preserving logic while testing the new animation state; responsive tests now accept the intentional 68px button and the SVG test targets its new animated group. A new opt-in run checks these corrections. No production release has been requested. Real iPhone Safari motion and VoiceOver remain unverified; Firebase production remains v2.3.8.

## CHG-20261106-475 | Repair the iPhone bottom scroll viewport beyond v2.3.7

**Owner evidence:** An actual 430px iPhone Plus screenshot of the installed v2.3.7 About page shows Version History cut at the bottom, with a visually empty dark strip beside the detached +. The earlier patch removed conditional child padding but missed the legacy `@media (max-width:760px) .dn-main { padding:0 14px 88px !important; }`. The parent is box-sized, so this 88px reservation reduces the inner scroll viewport on *every* portrait-mobile route. The header and fixed + remain full viewport height, causing the visible gap even when `.dn-command-controls` is empty.

**Correction:** When the new command root is mounted, remove only the retired parent bottom inset; retain horizontal padding and existing child scroll clearance based on whether search/Refine is present. This prevents the old navigation's reserved space from cutting off About, Dashboard, Settings, Story or searchable lists; it leaves the independently docked/draggable +, search, finance nested scroll, account and Guest records, PDFs and desktop shell untouched. The versioned Guest tour v2026.11.06.2 remains accurate and requires no reset. Expand Playwright geometry contracts across 320/390/430px, light/dark and 430×932 iPhone Plus About History scroll-to-last-release, with a dedicated opt-in `[verify-command-238]` workflow pinned to Ubuntu 24.04. **Evidence status:** Source-derived fix, CI execution pending. v2.3.7 remains current Firebase production, and this commit is not a release; physical Safari and VoiceOver remain unverified.

## CHG-20261105-423 | Repair intermittent 2.3.3 success-toast tap target during entry motion
- **Observed failure:** The authorized full release gate at `0bbd1325` (run 42059931537) completed 799/801 browser cases, failing the 320px light and 390px dark toast **View** action height checks (43.8469px and 43.7579px against 43.9px). Node tests (618), lint, dependency and documentation checks passed; build/offline/PDF/upgrade verification was skipped after the browser failure and no Firebase deployment was authorized by a passing gate.
- **Root cause and resolution:** The 2.3.3 success-toast rise animation applied `scale(.98)` to the whole toast, momentarily reducing its otherwise 44px action target. Retain the entrance movement and opacity, remove whole-toast scaling, and add an explicitly full-motion per-frame action-height assertion to the existing browser toast spec. The six existing width/theme cases, reduced-motion style and warning/error behavior remain intact.
- **Validation plan:** A new opt-in, **nondeploying** `[verify-233-toast]` GitHub Actions job runs just `e2e/iphone-toast-feedback.spec.js` on supported Ubuntu 24.04/Chromium. Its results remain **unverified** until the job completes; this patch is not evidence of a successful full 801-case release gate. Only a new exact-SHA full check may release version 2.3.3.
- **Compatibility and scope:** `OldVersion` only. No modification to main, app version, data schema, persisted records, original attachments, account/Guest isolation, backup, Story, PDF, or production Hosting. Guest tour 2026.11.04.5 already represents the same View action and needs no reset.

## CHG-20261104-408 | DN 2.3.2: remove Dashboard Task action sheet, animate filter pickup and authorize gated release

Added a press-to-lift and sibling-drop browser test verifying visual pickup, ordering, cancellation safety, and unchanged Guest records. Fresh exact-source GitHub Actions checks are required; an authored test or queued run is not a pass. Real iPhone Safari/VoiceOver and two-device account Task sync are not verified.
- **Authorized release:** The owner authorized OldVersion-only permanent Firebase release 2.3.2. The final commit carrying `[verify-full] [deploy-live]` starts independent exact-commit full pre-release tests and the production job, which cannot publish until the full gate succeeds. The production action additionally enforces audit, Node/lint/build, live-version monotonicity, Firebase credentials and exact Hosting readback. Any failure stops publication. No runtime application, version, scope, backup, finance, PDF, Task sync, Guest fixture or data changes.

This OldVersion-only candidate commit includes [verify-full] and [deploy-live] so the isolated full pre-release suite runs first; production workflow waits for an independently successful full run on the exact commit before Firebase Hosting publication. Geometry checks at 320/390/430px showed no document overflow, 44px status actions, individually rounded Task rows and no visible ellipses; keyboard ArrowRight opened the existing Edit/Cancel/Delete menu and Escape closed it. A direct content-row swipe in the integrated browser also opened the same action menu, and tapping the row opened Edit without changing local Task storage when cancelled. `NO_COLOR=1 npm test` passed **610/610**; ESLint, production build and documentation checks passed. The build reports existing mixed-import and >500kB chunk warnings. Focused Playwright regression assertions were updated, but Playwright tests could not launch: the local Chromium headless-shell install repeatedly left `headless_shell.exe` missing.

## CHG-20261104-383 | Restore a single Tasks main landmark
- **Issue and root cause:** The shared app shell already exposes the primary `<main>` landmark, but both the Guest Task list and signed-in unavailable state rendered another nested `<main>`. This made screen-reader landmark navigation ambiguous and contradicted the documented single-main shell structure.
- **Change and impact:** Replace only the Tasks page wrapper element with a `<div>` retaining the same `dn-tasks-page` class. Update existing Guest/mobile Tasks browser selectors and assert one main landmark for Guest and signed-in states. Routes, headings, styling, Task actions, state, records, preferences, finance, Story/PDF, attachments, and backup behavior are unchanged. The versioned Guest tour already covers Tasks; no fixture or version refresh is needed.
- **Executed validation:** Integrated browser inspection of the loaded Guest Tasks route confirmed one main (`.dn-main`), a `DIV.dn-tasks-page`, and no document-width overflow (browser-reported viewport 426px). A 17-route integrated-browser geometry sweep (excluding Insights) also reported one main and no document-wide overflow on every visited route, with no page errors; Story and Moment Activities were still loading at capture, so this is not full screenshot acceptance. `NO_COLOR=1 npm test` passed **610/610**; `npm run lint`, `npm run build`, and `npm run check:docs` passed. The build retained existing mixed-import and >500kB chunk warnings. Focused repository Playwright execution could not launch because the Playwright headless-shell executable was missing; attempted browser installation did not complete.
- **Limits/governance:** The signed-in informational state has a regression assertion but was not opened in the integrated browser. E2E assertions were updated but not executed in this environment. This does not claim VoiceOver or physical iPhone acceptance. OldVersion only; no data write, PR, version bump, main change or deployment.

## CHG-20261104-382 | DN 2.3 iPhone Moment navigation and multiline title correction
- **Audit evidence:** Prior 18-route Chromium phone screenshots showed the horizontally scrolling Moment workspace hiding Gallery and PDF Studio, while Moment listing titles clipped to a single line despite declared two-line clamping. Source analysis found a persistent 49-52px card-wide right gutter for a menu anchored only in the top-right and an earlier global `white-space: nowrap` on Moment titles.
- **Implementation:** At phone widths up to 580px, show five equal-width Moment workspace destinations (Story, Activities, visually named Finance, Gallery, PDF); retain full Transactions/PDF Studio ARIA labels, original links, current-state underline and 44px-plus targets. Reclaim card-wide menu gutter while retaining 44px safe menu space in the heading. Override legacy nowrap for Moment titles; use a 3-line mobile / 2-line desktop clamp. No changes to Activity cards, records, PDF generation, finance, filter behavior, attachment persistence or cloud scope. Public Guest tour already exercises all affected screens; no fixture or version reset necessary.

The final exact source/test SHA `5e045a53bd8df3be19e734ba947544cb007704b4` passed [21/21 synthetic Chromium tests](https://github.com/Divin-kumar/DN_APP_MAIN/actions/runs/41774757157) at mobile light/dark widths, with unchanged open/tap/swipe menus, shared photo Activity cards and Guest records. Final 320/390/430px screenshots inspected. Physical Safari/VoiceOver, real-account, full-release backup and production not verified.
- **Governance:** OldVersion only. No publish, PR, version bump or modification of main.

## CHG-20261103-352 | DN 2.3 PDF preview accuracy and reachable update control
- **Source evidence:** PDF customization's Update preview was the final control after all options, and its preview heading combined the current draft Story format with the previous rendered visual style when a PDF had pending changes. These are misleading or inconvenient on narrow phones.
- **Implementation:** Move the one Update preview action to a minimal sticky status row below the PDF customization heading, remove the redundant bottom action, and show a simple current-preview confirmation after rendering. Derive preview-format metadata from the last rendered options until updated. Preserve pending-warning, disabled download/fullscreen, keyboard control and responsive font behavior.
- **Downstream and tour:** PDF image/text exporter, options, page breaks, fonts, source memories, original attachments, Guest/account scope, backup bytes and settings persistence remain untouched. Existing public Guest tour already opens PDF Studio, so no sample regeneration is required.
- **Verification boundary:** Scoped synthetic mobile/desktop browser acceptance authored for update control placement, stale metadata, refreshed output and data immutability. Exact SHA CI and full browser regression/physical iPhone testing remain separate gates. Only OldVersion changed; no PR, main modification, release or deployment.

## CHG-20261103-340 | DN 2.3 owner Task/Refine/Dashboard UI direction (OldVersion only)
- **Owner direction:** No Task grouping; show every independent Task as one flat, sorted row. Task creation belongs only to the global universal +, not a duplicate header/empty-state button. Dashboard section order must be Tasks, Moments, Activities (Guest where independent Tasks exist). Finish in-scope UI and its browser QA before starting another development phase.
- **Implementation:** Remove Task Refine Group by choices and the Group key from the live Task list preference schema; previously saved Group values are ignored by the normalizing view reader without changing records or rewriting old backups. Use one-row Tasks with compact readable date/priority/context, a shared accessible SwipeActionCard menu for edit/cancel/restore/delete, confirmation for irreversible deletion, 44px controls, and existing one-tap completion. Keep Task editor/quick-create context links. Apply the existing common discovery-strip styling to search/Refine and show editable applied-filter chips and matching count.
- **Consistent Refine model:** All shared list Refine sheets and the Task sheet now share calmer theme-aware choice/sort/footer styling and explicit guidance. Draft-only edits, preview counts, Cancel/Escape, Reset and Show results continue to behave as before. Moments/Activities/Transactions retain their existing advanced AND/OR conditions and saved default schemas; no financial computation, CSV, search, filter semantics or Guest/account data is changed.
- **Dashboard:** Move the independent Tasks section to the first data slot after Dashboard greeting, followed by Moments and Activities in DOM, keyboard and visible order. ## CHG-20261103-339 | DN 2.3 Phase 4G: detached Guest Task links after confirmed parent deletion
- **Scope:** A successfully removed Guest Moment now detaches Moment/Activity links on its independent Tasks without deleting or changing the Tasks. A successful Guest Activity deletion detaches just its matching links, including historical merged Activity IDs; an Activity merged into a surviving alias-bearing Activity remains linked. The existing guarded Task repository performs one validated write for multiple Activity aliases. Guest tour Tasks remain isolated from personal Guest records; account Task writes remain disabled.
- **Safety:** Parent save/delete finishes before link cleanup, so a failed parent write cannot detach Tasks. If the Task collection is malformed, unavailable or quota-blocked, Tasks remain intact with their old links and render as unavailable where appropriate; successful Moment deletion is never falsely rolled back. Session guards prevent late account/Guest or demo changes from writing to the wrong workspace. Older backups, Story/PDF, financial records, historical entries and original files are unchanged.
- **Tests and evidence boundary:** Added alias-preserving repository Node tests and Event-store orchestration tests for post-save ordering, Guest Moment deletion and account isolation. Tests are authored, not claimed passing until the exact commit's CI is checked. Browser, real iPhone Safari, Firestore permissions and full 2.3 acceptance remain pending. Existing Guest sample data demonstrates linked Tasks and needs no version reset for this lifecycle-only correction. No release, app version bump, PR, main write or deployment.

## CHG-20261103-316 | DN 2.3.0 approved Phase 0 product plan and future-session handoff (planning only)
- **Owner request and governance (3 November 2026):** Preserve the approved DN 2.3.0 scope here for future sessions before development proceeds. This is an owner-approved planning baseline, not approval to deploy or change `main`. Repository `Divin-kumar/DN_APP_MAIN`; all writes explicitly to `OldVersion` only. No new branch, PR, main modification, merge, hosting release, app-version bump, data migration or destructive action is authorized by this entry. Source and the master remain authoritative for behavior that has actually been implemented.
- **Product direction:** Premium, calm iPhone-first UI/UX with minimal visual clutter; avoid adding features beyond the expressly approved 2.3.0 scope. Preserve existing business logic, stable IDs, dates and optional times, original photo files, account/Guest isolation, existing Activity relationships, transactions, Story and PDF semantics. Phase 0 planning is source-derived; live browser screenshots, physical iPhone/Safari verification and the deployed Firestore Rules are NOT verified.
- **Story contrast workstream:** Correct the remaining low-contrast future/upcoming/undated Story writing on dark shell surfaces, without changing light book-paper colors or historical story layouts. Existing `src/features/events/components/MomentStoryDesign.css` contains 2.2 paper and auxiliary text overrides; first reproduce the exact defect and identify computed colors/selectors. Run focused dark/light Journal, Diary and historical Task-style Story tests across Heritage/Editorial/Minimal and 320/390/430px widths. Do not claim a visual fix without an executed screenshot/browser check.
- **Shared Filter & Sort 3.0:** One lightweight Refine trigger opens a dedicated iPhone bottom sheet (desktop constrained equivalent), never an expanded inline listing panel. Sort controls are a single horizontally scrollable strip of small chips; first tap of a newly selected field sorts ascending, tapping the same field again reverses direction, without a separate direction arrow/button/dropdown. Filters below retain supported conditional editors and nested AND/OR combinations; Apply commits atomically and Cancel/Escape/backdrop dismisses without saving draft changes. Sticky action area, keyboard/focus/scroll-lock handling, no exposed horizontal scrollbar, slim applied-filter chips in one horizontal row plus `+N` overflow. Preserve original persisted states, list-search behavior, finance totals, CSV exports, and stored user/Guest filter defaults. The current `ConditionBuilder`/`useConditionFilters` writes immediately, so a truly isolated draft/commit adapter is required.
- **Grouping decision:** NO new Group By on Moments or Activities (the established Filter & Sort controls cover those needs); preserve simple continuous lists. **Only independent Tasks** may optionally group by Due Date (default), Moment, Activity, or Status, inside the Task Refine sheet. Lightweight headings with counts, no nesting or duplicated rows; filter first, then group, and sort within each group. Unlinked Tasks are Standalone and Moment-only Tasks must not be falsely linked to an Activity. Grouping is presentation-only with no hierarchy or migration.
- **Independent Tasks module:** Add a dedicated `/tasks` page, Task store and service/records with optional one-Moment and one-parent-Activity links, optional due date/time, status, priority and notes. Task completion stays independent of Moment and Activity status. Never migrate/rename/replace old Task-type Activities or delete historical Activity content; the existing historical Task-style Activity Story/PDF format remains available. **New** independent Tasks are excluded from Story and PDF exports, including when linked. Dashboard gets an upcoming/overdue Tasks section and inline completion after Moments and Activities; provide access from Dashboard and universal + while preserving the existing five-position mobile dock. Parent deletion/unlinking must never cascade-delete independent Tasks.
- **Settings redesign:** Replace crowded mixed accordion/embedded hub UI with a consistent compact Settings home and clear detail pages for Profile, Appearance, Story & PDF styles, Dashboard, Filter & Sort Defaults, Code Lists, Notifications (Coming Soon), Data & Backup, and About DN. **Owner expressly requires Code Lists and Filter & Sort Defaults on separate pages**: keep `/settings/code-lists` exclusively for reusable Code List values, and create `/settings/list-defaults` for saved filters and sorting across Moments, Activities, Transactions and Tasks. Extract the defaults editor currently embedded in `src/pages/CodeLists.jsx` without duplicating persistence. Preserve values and saved defaults, legacy `/settings/code-lists#list-order` links (redirect), titles, browser history/back navigation, existing stored settings and reset semantics. Respect light/dark, compact touch-safe controls and independent pages without adding new permanent navigation tabs.
- **Story/PDF quality:** Refine pagination/customization/readability without altering already accepted 2.2 behavior: original Activity as one chapter, its later dated/timed entries inside that chapter with continuous writing and no nested Entry cards; preserve independent same-day Activities and opt-in Natural flow/New day/New Activity page-break choices, image fit to available space when safe, photo originals, page numbering, searchable/high-resolution PDF controls, defaults and faithful preview/export. Preserve iPhone inline date/time and previous visual refinements. Do not conflate source Activity records with entry rows.
- **One Guest sample dataset, no extra playground:** Owner requested ONE comprehensive public Guest tour dataset sufficient for both realistic new-user exploration and minute functional testing, with **no separate QA Playground, second scenario selector or fixed upper item count**. It should cover every accessible module and combination: varied Moment types/statuses/recurrence, Journal/Diary/historical Activity styles, multiple entries/times/untimed, independent Tasks/link/group/status, original local images and mixed photo dimensions, long Story/PDF cases, income/expenses/Cash/Online/linked finance, AND/OR filters, sorting, Dashboard, Settings and backup. Favor natural writing/images and a clean initial list; test destructive/error states through safe fixtures or actions rather than exposing corrupt data to new users. Keep existing versioned `src/dev/sampleData.js` isolation; explicit Refresh demo is the sole destructive demo reset, warn before discarding demo edits, never overwrite personal Guest records or import demo into an account. Review/bump tour version and acceptance tests for every user-facing change.
- **Storage, privacy and migration impacts:** Source review identified `src/services/guestMergeService.js` Guest-to-account transfer, `src/services/backupService.js` v3 collection whitelist, `src/services/backupFileService.js` array-field stream support, `src/utils/listViewDefaults.js` strict saved-domain validation, `src/utils/accountCloudDeletionSafety.js` explicit collection inventory, account/Guest scope guards and demo originals. Introduce versioned backup support for independent Tasks (v4 proposed) while supporting valid older v3 backups; an older backup without Tasks MUST NOT erase existing Tasks. Include Tasks in explicitly authorized account deletion, local clearing, Guest/account merge and restored counts; preserve scope checks and partial-failure safeguards. Firestore security rules for a new account-owned Tasks collection were not found in the reviewed `OldVersion` tree, and deployed permission behavior is UNVERIFIED; verify before implementing cloud writes or declaring readiness.

Prefer focused relevant unit/Playwright tests per workstream, then exactly grounded full regression on final exact release SHA, not a full 616+ browser suite for every small fix.

## CHG-20261103-302 | Phase 4C long-book navigation and opt-in Activity page boundaries

## CHG-20261103-294 | DN 2.2 Phase 3A: Optional Unicode-searchable PDF text layer
- **Scope:** Owner authorized Phase 3 of the archival PDF upgrade after the passing Phase 2 fast CI. Add an opt-in searchable PDF format in Studio while preserving the current image-only PDF path by default. No package/lock changes or new remote/runtime dependencies.
- **Implementation:** `MomentPdfPainter` captures exact strings, A4 positions and font sizes drawn onto each PDF JPEG page only when the optional switch is enabled. The binary assembler retains the identical JPEG bytes and overlays invisible text using per-page Type1 Helvetica glyph maps with `/ToUnicode` CMaps, including UTF-16 surrogate pairs. No OCR, extra or rewritten dates, source record mutations or copied photos. The final PDF preview is still the same page JPEG; a style/text-mode edit invalidates download until the new PDF is generated.
- **Compatibility:** Standard page-image export remains byte compatible when the switch is off. Searchability is a beta enhancement, not vector text, font embedding, high-resolution photo printing or PDF/A compliance. Current Guest/account session and attachment scope guards stay in place. Errors fail closed without downloading stale output.
- **QA:** New Node unit tests inspect Unicode text mappings, PDF xref offsets, multi-page JPEG identity and standard fallback; authored Playwright checks verify the Studio checkbox, stale preview, download PDF objects, and unchanged Guest records. A separate local PDF experiment was independently extracted in PyMuPDF with Tamil and emoji; CI/browser/iPhone evidence must be reported by exact SHA, not inferred. Public tour version `2026.11.03.2` refreshes sample instructions on explicit Refresh demo; personal data is untouched.
- **Release:** OldVersion only, no version bump, no PR, `main` merge, Firebase/Vercel deployment or full-release skip. Full vector typography and photo print fidelity remain follow-on work.

## CHG-20261103-293 | DN 2.2.0 PDF Studio preview-first UI and real raster-style choices

Preserve all existing Journal/Diary/Tasks Story formats and data.
- **Output fidelity:** `visualStyle` is distinct from PDF `template`. `normalizePdfStudioOptions` inherits the per-format saved design; changing format re-inherits unless Studio has an explicit style override. `MomentPdfPainter` changes selected paper, ink, accent and typeface in the **actual JPEG PDF pages** while preserving the current PDF assembler, page count limits, photo lookups, page previews and session guards. Preview and PDF use the same JPEG page bytes; any edit/render error keeps downloads disabled until a successful refresh. No vector/text/PDF-A claim is made.
- **Compatibility:** Retain all previous options, preview labels, original attachment links, Activity text and nested writing behavior. Only PDF presentation settings and generated copy change; existing records, finance, external storage and downloaded PDFs are not modified. Current Studio overrides are never saved to Settings or a Moment.

## CHG-20261103-292 | Focused Settings SSR test-harness compatibility correction
- **Observed CI:** The first Phase 1 source commit `b3e77dd` passed documentation and lint, but its Node suite reported 10 failures, all from the existing Settings restore SSR harness importing the new visual picker as a real React component. No application UI, original records, PDF renderer or storage behavior is changed.

## CHG-20261031-248 | Block Firebase production publication on failed PWA upgrade preservation (OldVersion only)
- **Production gate audit:** The existing opt-in `dn-oldversion-firebase-production.yml` already required locked dependencies, moderate-level security audit, Node/lint, Chromium, Vite production build, built offline/original/PDF smoke and release-version check, but did not execute the prior-release upgrade preservation check that the normal branch CI runs. A red upgrade result could therefore have been overlooked during production dispatch.
- **Fail-closed fix:** Added the same pinned previous-release fixture archive/build and `npm run test:upgrade` as mandatory steps **before** Firebase service-account verification and deployment. ## CHG-20261031-236 | Mandatory Daily/Journal/Tasks creation and safeguarded legacy consolidation
- **Decision:** Every configured Moment type must select one of exactly three Activity modes. **Daily:** one Activity per local date per Moment, each new entry appended with its own title/text/time. **Journal:** default New creates separate memory; intentional Append selects an existing memory, and existing independent memories may be merged only with an explicit destination/source choice. **Tasks:** always creates independent Activities, no append or merge choice.
- **Configuration:** Moment Type Code Lists require one of the three modes before saving a new type. Legacy types normalize to the appropriate explicit mode (Diary => Daily; recognized task/project names => Tasks; all remaining names => Journal), while preserving custom types and prior Story presentation choices. New Moment creation requires a configured Moment type; legacy Other/inactive types remain editable.
- **Write paths:** Universal +, Moments chooser and Moment workspace all use the same append/create logic, scoped by parent Moment identity. Saved entries have their own IDs, selected date/time, rich text and creation metadata. Daily Activities retain one parent per date and display individually editable child entries in Detail. Journal can add new independent memories, append to a chosen memory, or intentionally merge two existing ones. Original Tasks work remains independent.
- **Existing data:** Once the correct account/Guest Code List scope is ready, the persistent shell automatically and idempotently consolidates legacy Diary Activities of equal local date **within the same Moment**, preserving the surviving Activity ID. It retains each historical title/text/time as a separate nested entry, remaps Moment photo references to the surviving ID with source entry ownership, keeps existing file originals, preserves nested expenses and stores removed Activity IDs as aliases. Undated historical records are not assigned fabricated dates. Journal legacy records are never auto-merged.
- **Downstream:** Story and PDF read the individual daily entries in time order and Journal nested notes; Journal original photos remain with the owning memory. Activity Detail has editable/deletable child entries; Diary completion/overdue indicators are suppressed. Search includes appended text. Old Activity deep links resolve to the surviving parent; Activity-detail transactions, global transaction cards, transaction relation edits, backup reconstruction/mirror precedence, and backup warnings resolve aliases without rewriting master ledger IDs. Existing v3 backup shape is retained.
- **iPhone UI:** Existing compact shared DropdownSelect, modal, semantic labels and search/Refine patterns were retained; contextual mode help and a lightweight Journal merge form were introduced with 44px pointer targets, 16px phone input font protection and reduced-motion support. No new permanent screen header, dock changes or visual clutter.
- **QA boundary:** Added pure Node tests for Diary, Journal, Tasks, photo/transaction provenance and v3 backup aliases, plus responsive Guest E2E scenarios and adjusted impacted older tests. Ten isolated utility regression assertions were actually executed and passed. Full Node suite, build, Playwright, physical iPhone Safari, multiple-device write collision and live-account restore **are not verified**: existing GitHub CI is still blocked at `npm audit --audit-level=moderate`. Check a backup before any real-data Firebase release and validate merged metadata and links. No Firebase/Vercel deployment, `main`, checkpoint, app version or new branch changes.

New page includes a 44px back link, current Guest/account context, small section jump links and responsive standalone layout.

Dialogs and persistence handlers are **shared**, not duplicated. No operation runs from navigation alone; Guest/demo cannot open cloud deletion. `src/pages/SettingsDataPremium.css` adds scoped light/dark treatment.
- **Useful guidance:** The new Data guide distinguishes cloud account documents, device-local original photo files and unencrypted downloaded JSON. A five-step disclosure explains safe export, file privacy, **preview a restore without committing** (then cancel), choosing target scope and the important distinctions between cloud/local clears and legacy Storage. No new automatic deletion, storage inspection, cloud file upload, encryption or paid services.

## CHG-20261031-230 | Premium Data & Backup, separate Firebase cloud deletion
- **Owner request:** Data & Backup currently only has Clear local. Add a separate action for deleting online Firebase data, with premium styling across Export, Restore, Clear local and the new cloud action. Do not actually clear any user's data.
- **Implementation:** `src/pages/Settings.jsx` and new `src/pages/SettingsDataPremium.css` separate safe backup/restore buttons from browser-local and signed-in cloud danger choices. Cloud deletion requires an explicit checkbox plus typing `DELETE MY CLOUD DATA`, an account-specific review dialog, and matching signed-in non-Guest identity. No accidental trigger on opening/closing; touch-friendly responsive light/dark controls.
- **Cloud service:** New `src/services/accountCloudDeletionService.js` + testable `src/utils/accountCloudDeletionSafety.js` enumerate Firestore `users/{uid}/{eventGroups,events,expenses,incomes,transactions,settings}` using server reads, reject unknown/foreign paths, require same Firebase/Auth/store identity, fail before any deletion on unreadable collection, read the permitted `settings/codelists` document directly, batch deletes at 200, and re-read all known scopes before reporting success. Once verified, sign out the active Firebase session before reload so it cannot immediately recreate cloud defaults; authentication identity is retained. A failed later batch may mean irrevocable **partial deletion**, disclosed to the user.
- **Important limits:** This deletes only known account-owned **Firestore records**, including legacy event and unified finance documents, **not** the Google/Firebase Authentication account, this browser's original attachment files, possible historic Firebase Storage blobs, or other users/Guests. DN may regenerate default Code Lists on login. No Cloud Functions, Firestore Rules changes or paid services added. Encourage a verified Export backup and close other tabs. The online action is hidden in Guest/demo.
- **QA:** Eleven isolated guard/algorithm scenarios executed against the exact fetched GitHub helper source (blob `026b81ee`), all passed; **not** a Firebase SDK, Node test suite, browser or complete build run. Seven source-authored `node:test` cases for auth and ownership, full collection coverage, preflight permission denied, foreign scope, mid-batch identity change, partial failure/verification and empty account; six 320/390/430px × light/dark Guest E2E cases check responsive premium button controls, online action hidden, local cancel and unchanged Guest storage. Browser, full CI/build, real signed-in Firebase and physical iPhone remain unverified. Any real deletion test must use a disposable account only.
- **Downstream:** Local Clear function unchanged, backup and restore logic unchanged, same record schema and existing photo originals, finance totals, PDFs, stored filters, nested Transactions scrolling, swipe and five-slot dock. No Guest tour fixture/version refresh needed. No user data deleted in development.
- **Release:** Implemented only on `DN-2.1-batch2` after CHG-229. Frozen Batch 1 `OldVersion`, `main`, app version and Firebase Hosting untouched. Separate owner authorization required before publishing Batch 2.

## CHG-20261031-225 | Fix sticky universal + color on iPhone without touching Batch 1
- **Owner report:** Screenshot from the freshly implemented Firebase iPhone navigation shows the centered + changes color after tapping and does not return until a tap elsewhere. Preserve the accepted five-position dock design.
- **Source diagnosis:** Mobile Safari may retain `:hover` following touch. The shared quick-create handler also unconditionally focused the + button after a menu action, leaving touch focus where keyboard focus was intended. Both can prolong an apparent pressed/selected style after menu transitions.
- **Implementation:** Scoped touch-only CSS in `src/layouts/PremiumMobileDock.css` forces the original gradient, white glyph, border and shadow under hovered/focused/open touch states, leaving keyboard `:focus-visible` intact. In `src/layouts/DashboardLayout.jsx`, touch toggles blur the + while keyboard toggles retain focus. Menu actions pass their click event to `beginQuickCreate` and restore focus to + only for keyboard activation (`event.detail === 0`). Escape still returns focus to +. No changes to create forms, callbacks, menus, business records, or dock geometry.
- **New tests:** Two synthetic iPhone touch cases compare computed style across repeat taps, menu action/cancel and outside tap in light/dark, and one keyboard test verifies Escape focus return. Files are source-verified **but not executed**. Physical iPhone Safari and full CI/build remain unverified.
- **Release isolation:** Owner actively implementing/testing Batch 1 CHG-213–224 from frozen `OldVersion` SHA `57a497e75356dcca64517efbe4790935463f4b61`. CHG-225 begins **Batch 2** on new `DN-2.1-batch2` branch based on that exact SHA. **No edits to OldVersion/main, no version bump, merge or Firebase release.** Batch 2 code will not appear in the currently tested Firebase release.

## CHG-20261031-223 | Prevent stale Transactions after Guest demo/account switches; Firebase-only workflow authority
- **Finding:** Financials combined Income and Expense ledgers when only their `loadedFor` matched the active account/Guest key, ignoring the stores' `activeLoadKey` and isolated-demo `activeSample`/`loadedSample`. Different Guest scenarios share a `guest:anonymous` scope. During fast account/sample transitions old transactions and totals could briefly render; an open transaction editor or delete dialog could survive an identity change.
- **Fix:** Introduce `isFinancialViewReady` requiring both ledgers to match the active and loaded scope, active/loaded sample, and collection shape before projecting transactions. Subscribe to sample changes in Finance loader effect and remount the Finance page's draft/dialog state on account/Guest/sample identity changes. Show the existing loading skeleton while two ledgers do not describe the same current workspace.
- **Downstream:** Preserves user-requested nested Transaction scrolling, compact three metrics, existing search and Refine conditions, swipe actions, transaction mutations, linked Moment rules and persisted Income/Expense records. No migrated data, local storage keys, PDF, Code Lists, backup format, original images or Guest tour version change. No Firebase deploy, version change or `main` edit.
- **QA executed:** Node 22 `node --test financialLoadScope.test.mjs` passed **8/8** assertions covering both prior ledger-load tests and two new scope/sample cases. Files were reproduced offline with exact GitHub blob hashes: helper `6d745bee21c59e288830138900e61714fbe1bf8b`, test `928c966747f0edcebeed6fc6a7c432a75c52c8f7`. This establishes isolated pure-logic checks only, not repo-wide CI. Three 320/390/430px browser tests were authored for foreign-sample totals/rows and Guest-storage preservation but **not executed**; signed-in switching, physical Safari, lint, build, backup and full exact-SHA release verification remain open.
- **Owner workflow:** Updated the existing `AGENTS.md` standing rule to honor latest Firebase-only acceptance, with no Vercel manual QA gate. OldVersion commits continue under standing authorization and are held for single owner-authorized Firebase batch.
- **Release:** CHG-223 appended to CHG-213–222; not present in the permanent Firebase app until an approved batch release.

## CHG-20261031-222 | Preserve Favorites and Recent when old backups omit Dashboard preferences
- **Finding:** Restoring any settings-only or older backup that omitted `dashboardConfiguration.favorites` or `recent` silently replaced the corresponding destination browser list with `[]`. Missing data was misinterpreted as an explicit instruction to clear the user's shortcuts.
- **Fix:** New `backupDashboardConfiguration.js` validates optional Dashboard shortcut arrays during schema parsing and constructs an import patch for only present fields. Restore writes only those fields for the initially captured Guest/account ID. A missing field preserves existing browser shortcuts, an explicit empty list clears that field, and existing Moment ID checks, deduplication and eight-entry Recent cap remain. Numeric legacy IDs are normalized. UI restore explanation clarifies the difference.
- **QA authored:** Four pure helper tests, two backup-schema tests, three iPhone Guest actual legacy-settings restore scenarios at 320/390/430px, and an explicit-empty-v3 backup restore scenario. Assert favorites/recent retained when absent, cleared when explicitly empty and Guest Moments/finance records preserved. **Tests not run.**
- **Downstream:** No business-record writes or changes to finance totals, source attachment bytes, original PDFs, saved List defaults/Refine, Moment recurrence, Guest tour fixture/version, app version or UI layout. No Firebase deployment, `main` edits or Vercel QA requirement. Historical 16-check owner acceptance remains tied to CHG-213–216 only.
- **Firebase batch:** Hold CHG-222 with CHG-213–221 for one separately authorized Firebase release, subject to exact-SHA CI, backup round-trip and physical iPhone testing.

## CHG-20261031-221 | Correct short-month and leap-day recurring Moment dates everywhere
- **Finding:** Monthly recurrence on day 29/30/31 overflowed into the next calendar month when the target month was shorter, and annual February-29 occurrences could display as March 1. The Activity Moment picker independently duplicated the flawed calculation, diverging from the Dashboard's `getNextOccurrenceDate`.
- **Fix:** Use calendar-month end clamping in the shared `getNextOccurrenceDate` function so each occurrence independently preserves the original day where valid. A Jan-31 monthly Moment occurs Feb-28/29 then Mar-31; Feb-29 annual anniversaries occur Feb-28 on non-leap years. Preserve the original start-date gate. Replace the Moments page's local `getTimelineDate` with the shared helper so Activity picker suggestions and Dashboard share the calculation. Leave all persisted Moment dates/times unchanged.
- **Validation authored:** Five Node test cases for boundary dates, 2028 leap year, recurrence semantics, calendar-day Dashboard inclusion, birthday age and negative UTC timezones. Three iPhone Playwright Guest cases for the Activity picker date display on 320/390/430px. No automated/browser tests executed here. The existing manual 16/16 feedback covers only earlier CHG-213–216 and does **not** certify this addition.
- **Downstream:** The corrected recurrence is presentation/calendar arithmetic. No original file, record schema, activity/expense/income entry, PDF, code-list preset, saved filter state, Guest data/guide or tour version changed. Date-only and optional time fields remain intact. No version increment, Firebase deploy, `main` mutation or Vercel dependency.
- **Firebase batch:** Append CHG-221 to the cumulative CHG-213–221 queue for one future owner-approved Firebase deployment; include calendar boundary review in consolidated testing.

## CHG-20261031-217 | Saved Settings sort and filter defaults are now included in full backups
- **Finding:** CHG-214 made Settings the source of List defaults, but the existing application JSON backup exported records, Code Lists and other preferences without those defaults. A restore to another browser would silently lose the chosen Reset baseline.

Export never copies the working page sort/search/Refine state. Restore validates all three domains and bounded filter trees at parsing time, then reapplies saved defaults to the destination account/Guest browser scope only after record import and original-file restoration succeed. The existing live page view remains unchanged.

Signed-out exports omit it. Invalid saved-default keys, sort choices and filter trees fail preflight rather than silently importing a corrupted baseline.
- **UX:** Data & Backup's explainer and restore review explicitly disclose included saved baseline behavior and what old files preserve.
- **Validation authored:** New utility tests for cross-scope roundtrip, bad schema/tree, missing optional data and non-mutated working lists; v3 backup schema tests for extension and legacy; synthetic 320/390/430px Guest browser download checks exported JSON sort and condition data. Those cases were **not executed** here; exact-SHA build/lint/unit/browser, live preview, physical iPhone Safari and real signed-in backup restore remain **Unverified**.

## CHG-20261031-215 | Make saved List defaults directly accessible from Settings
- **Problem:** After separating saved filter defaults from working list state, those important preferences were still located beneath six long Code List sections; users had to traverse the configuration taxonomy to reach them.
- **Change:** Give Settings a distinct premium, 44px+ direct `List defaults` navigation card linked to `/settings/code-lists#list-order`. Code Lists now scrolls its real `.dn-content` ancestor to the existing saved-default section after route layout, using standard `scrollIntoView({ block: 'start', inline: 'nearest', behavior: 'auto' })`; no new route or duplicate form. ## CHG-20261030-214 | Settings now owns saved defaults, not live list filters
- **Owner requirement:** Settings-configured filters and sorting must be authoritative defaults, not a mirror of active lists. Reset in Activities must restore the current Settings choice. Owner also requested a premium Settings UI and a cumulative later Firebase deployment batch.
- **Architecture:** Replace the former shared live/Settings storage keys with scoped `defaults:moments`, `defaults:activities`, `defaults:transactions` and matching `conditions:defaults:*` keys. Current page keys remain untouched for compatibility. A shared `useListViewDefaults` supplies baseline sort/conditions and safe initial-page defaults; `useConditionFilters` adds an explicit startingGroup for truly fresh lists and an atomic restore method that preserves migration version. Preserve existing working filters rather than overwriting them when Settings changes.
- **Behavior:** Refine Reset and the no-match recovery restore saved Settings filters plus sorting for the three main lists. Reset no longer means Clear all; the recovery label is `Restore saved defaults`. Built-in defaults remain available from each settings editor, and Reset all settings includes saved baselines after its existing scoped-record checks. Nested per-Moment filters remain independent.
- **Visual:** Create a calmer editorial default-baseline introduction, three compact sortable cards, a separate default-filter editor with automatic-save explanation and a `Restore built-in defaults` action. The existing premium search/Refine and photo cards remain unchanged.
- **Firebase batching:** CHG-213 and CHG-214 are **pending the owner's future batch deployment**. No Firebase publication/version bump was requested or executed in this change. Keep tracking subsequent changes from this owner-approved checkpoint; public source-parity version still requires independent verification. Vercel build rate remains limited, and `main` stays untouched.
- **QA/limits:** Added synthetic unit and 320/390/430px iPhone browser cases for separate defaults, page sort/filter reset, new versus legacy working preferences, scope isolation and preserved Guest data. Updated old empty-state recovery labels and corrected the earlier pending Activity fixture expectation. Source readback and commit are not an executed build/test. Full exact-SHA Node/ESLint/Chromium/offline/PDF/upgrade, physical Safari and real signed-in scope are **Unverified**. Guest demo fixtures were reviewed and remain representative; no silent refresh or tour-version bump.

Added synthetic Playwright coverage for 320/390/430px light/dark on both routes, filtered-empty recovery, first-use variants, 44px targets, no overflow, data preservation and no page errors. Complete exact-SHA CI, fresh deployment review and physical Safari/VoiceOver are **Unverified**, not asserted passed.

## CHG-20261030-204 | Resolve last blocked-preview CI geometry checks without diluting premium UI
- **Evidence:** Verified latest completed `OldVersion` GitHub browser run on `d73d4ac`: **358 passed, 2 failed** (improved from 333/21). Remaining failures: a genuine **28px** horizontal shift in the Refine direction/Reset rail after changing Moment sort labels; and a Code Lists input measured `43.999755859375px` against an exact 44px assertion at 430px dark.
- **Root cause:** Refine's panel is flex-column with an inherited `align-items: start` from its earlier grid styling. The action-line therefore shrink-wraps to the selected dropdown label despite its internally fixed grid columns. Stretching that action-line to the containing panel fixes the cross-page right edge while preserving intrinsic dropdown length, 44px touch targets, compact Asc/Desc and Reset and the one-line layout.
- **Change:** Constrain only the shared `ListControls` action-line to `align-self: stretch; width:100%; min-width:0; box-sizing:border-box`. Strengthen the E2E geometry check to assert a stable action-strip width and position across selected sort labels. Code Lists CSS continues to set a 44px input minimum, but its test now tolerates a sub-0.1px browser rounding artifact, while retaining alignment, 16px type and Add-button target requirements.
- **Owner review register:** Append CHG-204 to `docs/MASTER_SOURCE_OF_TRUTH.md`; retain CHG-198 through CHG-203 as **not owner-tested** due to Vercel's build-rate limit. Request no owner screenshots until a current review build is available.
- **Downstream:** Shared sort panel affects Moments, Activities, Moment Activities, Transactions and any other ListControls consumer; tests cover the common component on these routes. No sorting/filter logic, saved choices, transactions, data model, original files, Guest fixture/version, account scope, release version or production behavior changed.
- **Verification:** Prior CI results and source reviewed. The new commit's full browser, lint, build and iPhone Safari checks remain unverified until their actual results are available. No Vercel plan upgrade, Firebase deployment or `main` modification.

## CHG-20261030-177 | Preserve all seeded fields through Activity swipe QA normalization
- **Finding:** OldVersion CI run 41027334545 completed 480/480 Node tests and 207/213 Chromium journeys. Income and expense JSON remain byte-identical.

No Firebase or main changes.

## CHG-20261030-174 | Keep swipe gestures alive through outside pointer release; update Activity density contract
- **Evidence:** CHG-173 exact-SHA CI (run 41021861756) passed Node, lint, docs and six new phone-width Activity typography cases; eight cases remained, including seven rightward/repeated pointer swipes whose popup was absent and one stale 820px Activity height threshold (119.98px against 115px). ## CHG-20261030-171 | Unify iPhone Activity swipes into explicit, direction-neutral popup actions
- **Reason:** Activity overview and Home still completed/reopened a record on a left drag, contrary to the owner's desired same lightweight action popup for both swipe directions across DN. Accidental completion was possible.
- **Change:** Replace completion-on-drag wrappers on overall Activities and Home with the existing SwipeActionCard action popup in either direction. Offer Edit only for editable records, Complete/Reopen, Cancel/Restore and confirmation-gated Delete; retain ordinary tap-to-Detail, menu-free overall Activity card visuals, Home's keyboard-accessible options button and Left/Right Arrow menu access on overall cards. Make the otherwise dormant overall editor reachable and remove title autofocus on phone widths.
- **Downstream:** All actions use existing scoped record handlers and retention rules, including date/time, status metadata, backup v3, account/Guest separation, finance mirrors, original attachments, Story/PDF exports, filters/sort and detail routes. The old Home Ctrl/Command+Enter direct mutation is intentionally replaced by explicit menu selection. The unused SwipeToComplete primitive and its unit coverage are retained; other screens are not altered.
- **Guest demo:** Existing pending/completed/cancelled tour records illustrate the popup without new fixture data; no GUEST_TOUR_VERSION bump or silent user reset.
- **Verification:** Update browser contracts for click, keyboard/menu, synthetic swipes/touch and explicit status mutations; add 320/390/430px light/dark popup, cancel/restore, edit and Delete-confirmation checks. Full exact-SHA lint, unit, Chromium, build, offline/PDF, upgrade and documentation CI plus Vercel status are required before acceptance. Native Safari/VoiceOver and actual signed-in cloud remain unverified.
- **Safety:** Only OldVersion review, no version bump, Firebase deployment or main writes.

## CHG-20261030-170 | Make the iPhone Home screen visibly lighter and more useful above the fold

Reduce responsive minimum height to 104px on phones and 108px on larger viewports, with auto height and wrapping for long names; retain theme contrast and existing list controls.

- **Guest demo:** Reviewed the current tour in `src/dev/sampleData.js`. Its existing three Moments and mixed Activities already exercise the first-screen density; no new feature story/data or tutorial revision is needed. Existing edited Guest records remain untouched.

Full exact-commit lint, Node, Chromium, build, offline/PDF, upgrade and documentation CI, plus Vercel status, are required after push. ## CHG-20261030-169 | Prevent premature phone keyboard in Moments-list Activity chooser
