- **Verification:** New synthetic Chromium checks at 320/390/430px, dark/light, cover no implicit title focus, explicit tap focus, 16px input typography, Date visibility, cancel, no overflow and byte-for-byte unchanged Guest data. Full exact-SHA Node, lint, browser, build, offline/PDF, upgrade and documentation checks are required after push. Native installed-iPhone Safari keyboard and zoom remain unverified.

## CHG-20261030-168 | Restore direct Add Transaction CTA under compact three-column Finance totals

Move "All income and expenses" to its own single, full-width subtitle row. Preserve three equal summary columns and the universal + menu. Update the source contract to require the CTA and subtitle rather than prohibit them.

No new stores, record writes, schema migration, financial totals changes, filter/sort changes, PDF or backup format changes. iPhone 320/390/430px, dark/light, tap sizing, no-wrap subtitle, heading geometry and cancellation with preserved Guest records are covered by `e2e/iphone-finance-entry-phase1.spec.js`. Regular account entry uses the same existing `openAdd` handler, to be independently assessed in the full CI suite.

Existing samples can exercise it without changing `GUEST_TOUR_VERSION` or unexpectedly refreshing local Guest records.

All code changes limited to `OldVersion`; no Firebase publish, version bump or `main` changes.

## CHG-20261030-167 | Keep shared swipe popup clear of the actual iPhone dock
- **Cause:** `SwipeActionCard` previously passed a fixed 80px bottom reserve to `computeSwipeMenuPlacement` on narrow viewports. The DN 2.1 bottom dock is at least 66px high and positioned 17px or a larger Home Indicator inset above the edge, meaning 80px could be insufficient and a menu could overlap the dock. When keyboard panning hid the dock, the same reserve could waste valuable visible space.
- **Change:** Introduce `getSwipeDockReserve` as a pure helper based on the dock's rendered rectangle and current visual viewport; use it only when the dock is visible and intersects that viewport. Account for an 8px gap. Keep the existing popup placement algorithm and viewport bounds, direction-neutral swipe-to-popup behavior and confirmation-gated Delete semantics.
- **Direct/downstream:** Shared `SwipeActionCard` is used by Moments, Activities, Transactions and Code Lists, including Activity Detail. Cross-module popup positioning changes only; no route, Activity status, financial totals, account/Guest scope, timestamps, Story/PDF, source attachments, backups or preferences are rewritten. Read-only Guest example review: no new sample or tutorial behavior and no demo revision needed.
- **Verification:** New pure tests cover 34px-style Home Indicator dock geometry, desktop/keyboard-hidden dock and visual viewport offsets. New synthetic Chromium regression at 320/390/430px injects 34px inset and checks Activity popup/dock separation, Escape and exact Guest data preservation. Existing swipe gesture/unit/browser suites plus full CI, Vercel review, built offline shell, upgrade check and documentation contract remain mandatory. No physical iPhone outcome claimed before review.
- **Safety:** OldVersion only, no release marker/version bump, `main` frozen and Firebase untouched.

- **Finding:** First P1 dock Chromium run at c2348e5 passed 179 existing browser journeys but failed three new navigation cases because the test kept querying the button by its original accessible name after the menu opened. Actual UI intentionally changes `Open quick create menu` to `Close quick create menu`. This was a test expectation defect, not evidence of a broken menu or CSS geometry.
- **Correction:** Update `e2e/iphone-mobile-dock-phase1.spec.js` to assert the correct accessible name and `aria-expanded` in each state, retaining Escape dismissal, route, sizing, clearance, overflow and unchanged Guest-record assertions. Existing CSS, menu, routes, state and keyboard focus are unchanged.

Check intentional (not forced) input focus, visible 16px+ editable controls to prevent focus-zoom regression, 44px close targets, cancel without writes, no horizontal overflow and no page errors. This protects the same universal entry path used across Dashboard, Moments, Activities and Transactions. Browser simulation does not prove installed-iPhone Safari keyboard geometry, real account sync or VoiceOver.
- **Data/sample/release:** Tests use isolated Guest seed data only and cancel drafts. No production code, persistence, finance, Story/PDF, original bytes, backup v3 or Guest sample catalogue was modified; no demo version bump required. `main` and Firebase remain untouched, with no `[deploy-live]` marker.
- **Verification:** Previous first dock run failed only the three test assertions (179 other browser journeys passed). Exact-SHA CI and Vercel review for the correction plus new tests must be checked before any full-pass claim.

## CHG-20261030-164 | Block older and duplicate Firebase versions before upload
- **Cause:** Production was reported at v2.0.5 while `OldVersion` remains v2.0.3. The legacy authenticated deployment auto-increment would generate v2.0.4 from that checkout; the opt-in GitHub production workflow previously had no live-version downgrade check. Neither path should overwrite a newer installed PWA with an older version.
- **Change:** Introduce pure semantic version and service-worker identifier helpers (`scripts/release-version-guard.mjs`), a fail-closed public live-version check (`scripts/check-hosting-release-version.mjs`), and dedicated Node tests. The legacy deploy performs the check **before** modifying package/lock files, building or publishing. The `OldVersion` production workflow checks the intended package version **before** the service-account/publication steps. Candidate must be strictly newer; same, older, malformed, unreachable or unrecognized live versions block.
- **Direct/downstream:** No change to deployed Firebase, `main`, application UI or backend, PWA update semantics, account/Guest records, finance, attachments, backup v3, Story, PDF or stored preferences. Existing Vercel preview workflow remains non-publishing. The owner must still separately approve a valid new release. The public Guest tour needs no update because no reachable application UI/demo interaction changed.
- **Verification:** Source-derived control-flow review and Node test cases for 2.0.4 vs 2.0.5, duplicates, 2.1.0 vs 2.0.5, malformed labels and generated worker source. Fresh post-change CI results are pending; the audit cannot claim that a real release has been attempted or a physical PWA verified.
- **Safety:** All changes on `OldVersion`; no `[deploy-live]` marker, no production publication, no package version bump, no owner data access. A live read error is a hard release stop; bypass requires new owner-governed release work rather than an unsafe automatic fallback.

## CHG-20261030-163 | Reconcile Firebase v2.0.5 label with OldVersion v2.0.3 review baseline
- **Authority:** Owner observed v2.0.5 on permanent Firebase, v2.0.3 on Vercel review, and requested reconciliation and continuation without unapproved live changes.
- **Source-derived cause:** Committed `OldVersion` package and lockfile declare v2.0.3. `vite.config.js` embeds that package version into About and the service worker. Legacy `scripts/deploy-hosting.mjs` increments the package/lock patch before deploying, explaining how a manual Firebase build may display a newer version without a matching committed version. The next legacy release from source v2.0.3 would be v2.0.4, below owner-reported production v2.0.5, so that path is not safe to run as-is.
- **Read-only verification:** `OldVersion` source SHA 88c59c1cc41d0409a2bbb03ce123ec2c7b38773c had successful GitHub Actions run 41003634021 for application smoke tests, documentation contract, unit, lint, Chromium, production shell, PDF/originals and upgrade checks. The Vercel review deployment for the same SHA is READY. Production v2.0.5 is owner-reported; exact Firebase build source/assets were not independently retrieved at the time of this change.
- **Change:** Add `scripts/audit-live-builds.mjs`, a public-only index/service-worker version and entry-fingerprint inspector for Firebase versus OldVersion review; run it as an explicitly non-gating, non-release CI step and include its evidence or access limitations in the workflow summary. Document separate source and production version provenance. Do not change package version or publish.
- **Direct/downstream:** No runtime UI, persistence, financial calculations, Date/Time, Story/PDF, backup v3, attachment bytes, routes, scope, Guest/account records or sample fixtures changed. Reviewed `src/dev/sampleData.js` obligations: read-only build auditing has no demo changes or version reset requirement. Further DN 2.1 UI implementation may continue on OldVersion; do not label this production parity or physical iPhone proof.
- **Verification:** Existing exact-SHA GitHub CI and Vercel READY status are **Executed/observed**. New read-only script and workflow require fresh post-change CI confirmation. Access to both public shells, semantic equivalence and physical installed-PWA behavior remain **Unverified** until independently checked. Release version alignment, `main`, Firebase and any irreversible data action were not performed.

## CHG-20261030-161 | Stabilize animated Date/Time alignment measurement without changing UI
- **Cause:** The first DN 2.1 safe-area CI run on `OldVersion` c13d23b passed all three synthetic safe-area cases and 175/176 Chromium journeys, but existing `e2e/all-date-times.spec.js` at 1280px sampled a date/time vertical difference of 3.363px during modal opening against its existing <2px requirement. The 1280px layout is outside the mobile-only safe-area rules, so this is not evidence of a new iPhone geometry defect.

Keep Date/Time width thresholds and all save/edit/clear/filter/order assertions unchanged; do not modify UI, styles, records, product behavior, Guest fixtures or document contract. If a steady-state misalignment exists, the test still fails.

## CHG-20261029-159 | Default new-entry clocks to the current local time, keeping manual Clear time
- **Authority/base:** Owner asked whether new entries automatically take the current time and can be cleared when unnecessary. The previous implementation at reviewed OldVersion d94b16ea5553ee0db2c884169ef6ec76ce03045b started new drafts empty. Implement current-time defaults for new Moments, Activities and Transactions, retaining source 2.0.3 and the standing OldVersion/Vercel review workflow.
- **Behavior/source-derived:** currentEntryTime formats the browser-local hour/minute, including midnight, without UTC conversion. New Moment and Transaction form initialization and new Activity entry points (universal Quick Create, Moment workspace and retained Moment chooser) capture that value when opened/reset for a fresh entry. An open draft does not tick or replace typed/cleared values. Reopening captures the new clock. Existing-record editing initializes from the saved clock, including missing/empty values; it does not stamp current time onto old records, including on the first render. Initialize each form from its record and reset only when its record/preset changes, so late-loaded default preferences cannot erase typed fields or refill a cleared clock; the wider browser run exposed this reset race. Clear time retains the existing UI and save semantics. No change to automatic Created/Completed metadata, date matching/order, finance calculations, IDs, scope guards, original files or backup v3.
- **Guest/documentation:** Guest edition 2026.10.29.4 adds current-time/default/Clear guidance while preserving its fixed example clocks, record IDs, amounts and illustrations. Edited tours update only through explicit Refresh demo; personal Guest/account records are not reset. Master, README, business guide and About explain capture-at-opening, non-ticking drafts, clearing and untimed edits. Earlier audit entries are unchanged.
- **Executed:** On Node 22.23.4, all 474 Node tests and the clean full 173-journey Chromium suite pass, with lint and production build. New controlled-clock journeys in Asia/Kolkata at 320/1280px check current local defaults for Moments, Activities and Transactions, fixed values while an open draft’s clock advances, current values on reopening, manual clear surviving edits/save, deliberate late preference changes preserving text/empty clocks and existing untimed record edits. Existing time create/edit/clear/chronology, all-filter matching totals, scope isolation, responsive forms, Story/PDF/original, backup and failure/retry cases pass. Unit coverage includes local midnight, padded hours/minutes and end-of-day formatting. The actual public Guest tour/version-refresh and private Guest protections pass in the full suite; its fixed sample clocks and original illustrations remain appropriate. Built-shell checks pass for offline Story, exact 68-byte original download, valid PDF, unchanged records and no page errors. Both previous-OldVersion upgrade paths preserve records/preferences/original bytes and avoid repeated prompts. Five-document inventory, 77 local links, all 158 historical entries and diff checks pass. Existing bundle-size warning remains.
- **Boundary:** OldVersion/Vercel only, no version increment, main untouched and no Firebase release marker/publication. Physical iPhone/Safari, installed PWA, real-account cloud authorization and deployed UI behavior remain unverified. Final source/CI/Vercel status is reported separately.

## CHG-20261029-157 | Configurable payment methods and transaction totals that follow every filter
- **Authority/base:** Owner reported missing Payment method in transaction filters, requested Income/Expense/Balance to follow the selected filters, and asked to manage dropdown choices through Settings for future use. Reviewed OldVersion base 159bc23cadf91d27c512f234dcce3520e00e4b3b includes the owner’s 2.0.3 package/lock update; preserve it without another version increment. Existing screenshot-defined configuration rows, side-by-side editable applied filters and hidden scrollbars remain unchanged.
- **Matching totals:** Global and Moment Transactions summarize exactly the same matching rows after search and the full condition expression, including payment method, type, category, amount, date, linked records and retained AND/OR groups. Expense-only results show zero income and a negative balance; empty results show zeros. Sorting changes order only. Matching captions identify this cohort. Activity Detail has no transaction filters and retains its full linked totals. Remove the now caller-free date-only summary projection; retain legacy filter migration and group semantics. Ledger deduplication, amounts, IDs and financial relationships are unchanged.
- **Configurable choices:** Add Payment method to both transaction filter catalogs and the shared Code Lists filter editor; add an editable Payment Methods section seeded with Cash/Online. Forms accept active configured choices and can retain an existing transaction’s retired/custom method. Read normalization preserves custom names in income/expense, linked mirrors, kind conversion and cards. Not specified/Specified comparisons handle missing historical methods without assigning one. Search also includes payment names. All editable filter domains combine active scoped Code Lists with historical record values: Moment Types, Income Types, Expense Categories, Activity Priorities and Payment Methods. Configured labels are shown while raw stored values remain stable. Status/visibility stay derived and linked Moment/Activity/time choices stay record-driven. Notifications shows Coming Soon; existing notification fields (including unknown values) and Reminder Presets remain saved/backed up, and ordinary preference saves preserve them. New-entry date defaults use the local calendar day; birthday turning age includes today correctly and workspace countdown shares the existing recurrence convention. A production-only offline Analytics startup rejection was reproduced and fixed by skipping optional initialization offline, including a transition during lazy import/support checks.
- **Guest/documentation:** Tour edition 2026.10.28.4 now describes Story, Gallery, PDF Studio and existing recovery guidance. Existing edited editions wait for explicit Refresh demo; private Guest data is never silently reset. Current master, business guide, About and README match the final destinations/availability. Exactly five authorized Markdown files remain; all 146 earlier audit entries are preserved verbatim.
- **Executed:** Final 454 Node tests and all 148 Chromium browser journeys pass; lint, clean npm install, production build, diff checks and documentation governance pass. Coverage includes 20 light/dark viewport combinations (320–1920px), complete scoped Moment/Activity/transaction journeys, route/back/forward links, dates/writing/status, file integrity, gestures/keyboard/focus, loading/error/retry/empty states, persistence, Code Lists and account/Guest/sample boundaries through synthetic fixtures. Actual Settings backup above 50 MiB, write-free preview, cancellation/reselection and exact-original round trip pass. A 68-state/17-route screenshot survey at 320/390/768/1280px found no page errors, horizontal overflow or record changes; all four PDF views were re-inspected after the tablet correction. Repeatable npm run test:production serves the built app locally and confirms worker activation plus offline Story, a byte-identical original download and genuine PDF export, without page errors or record changes. These are local results, not Firebase publication or browser verification of a Vercel deployment.
- **Dependency review/open limits:** Applied the compatible source-map-js 1.2.2 lockfile patch and verified it after npm ci. npm audit still reports 8 affected package entries (5 high, 3 moderate, 0 critical): Firebase/Firestore transitive gRPC, Vite/esbuild and React Router; no forced downgrade or major framework migration was folded into regression cleanup. These entries remain follow-up before claiming a fully cleared release. Physical iPhone/iPad/installed PWA, Safari/WebKit (missing host libraries), real-account cloud authorization and cross-device recovery remain unverified. Existing browser storage/memory, per-original limits and raster-PDF characteristics still apply. Commit/Vercel status is reported separately for this change.

## CHG-20261028-146 | Phase 7 incremental v3 backup transport without the fixed 50 MiB cap
- **Approved scope:** Owner authorized the next agreed phase after Phase 6. Remove the fixed application backup cap, improve large-file handling, retain existing JSON/legacy compatibility and verify originals/relationships without silent omission. No archive format, dependency, database migration or backup version change; serialized backups remain v3 JSON with inline base64 originals.
- **Transport/UI:** New backupFileService emits per-record/per-original JSON Blob parts and reads selected File slices instead of one full-file string. Import decodes/verifies one original at a time, retaining trusted descriptors/byte ranges in memory for confirmed restore; preview makes no original/record writes. Settings shows progress and cancellation before restore. Session/abort guards, complete-download-only behavior, existing merge/configuration policy and failure-safe Guest tour exit remain. About/Settings no longer advertise a 50 MB cap. Browser resources, largest record/value, Blob/download limits, destination quotas and the existing 10 MiB per-original bound remain practical limits.
- **Integrity refinement disclosed during review:** The old validator rejected reused child IDs across different Moments, and expense reconstruction grouped by child ID alone. Child uniqueness now uses Moment/child pairs for Activities, Memories and attachment references. Expense mirrors use that pair, retaining sole-owner inference for legacy parentless links; ambiguous/mismatched links remain ledger entries with preview warnings. Stored IDs/data are not rewritten. Canonical JSON validation still requires base64; descriptor-only runtime objects need the separately verified file index. Malformed/truncated JSON, duplicate root fields, unsupported versions, missing/corrupt originals and bad required relationships reject before confirmation.
- **Guest maintenance:** Bumped GUEST_TOUR_VERSION to 2026.10.28.3 and added recovery-backup/progress/cancellation guidance to the existing Journal introduction. Three Moments, fourteen Activities and sixteen originals remain; edited older editions wait for explicit Refresh demo and private Guest records are untouched.
- **Executed:** All **453 unit tests, 141 Chromium browser journeys, lint and production build passed** on the final application source. Includes actual Settings export above 50 MiB (six 7-MiB originals), write-free preview and confirmed original-hash round trip, 320px cancellation/reselection, explicit-only demo refresh, corrupt/missing originals, reused child IDs/expense isolation, v2/settings-only compatibility, leading UTF-8 BOM and deterministic JSON.parse parity across varied nested values/chunk boundaries. Final 320px backup/review-dialog inspection reported no page errors or horizontal overflow. Documentation governance passes with five approved documents, 77 local links, all 144 historical entries preserved and two new entries; diff checks pass. These are local test results; Vercel/GitHub status is reported separately for the resulting commit.
- **Boundary:** OldVersion source for Vercel review under standing authorization. No Firebase publish, main change, version bump or Phase 8/9 implementation. Physical iPhone/PWA, real-account cloud and arbitrary-size recovery are not established by synthetic browser tests.

## CHG-20261028-143 | Phase 6 review: consistent customized previews, complete Journal photos and section pagination
- **Scope and reproduced findings:** Owner authorized Phase 6 review/refinement. Reviewed OldVersion e0bee938 and traced PDF Studio, both summary launchers, scoped original reads, Guest tour fixtures and Settings restore. Browser reproduction found Code List refresh silently exported default options while controls retained customization, a missing first cover original prevented fallback to readable owned photos, and Journal omitted attachments from undated/upcoming entries. Visual review of the public Guest Journal also found Earlier reflections stranded without its writing at the page foot.
- **Corrections:** Source refresh preserves selected PDF options and keeps defaults aligned before customization. Cover selection tries readable originals from included sections, omits only a successfully featured reference from the body, and keeps unavailable references identified with their owner. Journal renders photos and other-file labels for undated and selected upcoming entries, without inventing dates or changing ownership. Studio section pagination measures heading height and reserves room for following writing; the legacy summary's spacing policy is retained.
- **Executed:** Baseline 441 unit tests and eight focused PDF/Guest browser checks passed before edits. After refinement, all 441 unit tests, lint, production build, 12 focused PDF/Guest browser checks and the full **138 Chromium browser tests** pass. Four new browser regressions verify customized preview/download JPEG parity after Code List refresh, missing-cover fallback, actual drawn photos for undated/upcoming entries (and photo-free Diary/Tasks), and heading/body placement on real public-demo PDF canvases. Rendered and visually inspected 18 pages across Journal, Diary, Tasks and a long-text/missing-original/undated/future-photo stress export. Documentation governance passes with exactly five approved documents, 75 local links and all 142 historical entries preserved; diff whitespace checks pass. These are local results, not a GitHub CI or deployed-preview claim.
- **Guest tour maintenance:** Reviewed src/dev/sampleData.js and exported the public tour. Fixtures and GUEST_TOUR_VERSION remain unchanged because formats, controls and demonstrated content are unchanged; corrected edge cases use focused synthetic regressions, and the reflections check uses the actual public demo. Existing Guest restore success/failure/ledger safeguards remain covered. No edited tour or personal Guest records are reset by this work.
- **Boundary:** Local refinements on OldVersion; no commit, push, main change, deployment, version bump, schema/backup migration or Phase 7 work. Owner PDF visual acceptance, deployed-preview verification, physical iPhone/PWA and real-account Firebase verification remain outstanding. Documentation updates are confined to the existing master, business guide and audit log.

## CHG-20261028-141 | Fix Guest backup restore from tour and simplify Guest data switch
- **Owner report:** The Use my Guest data control was too visually prominent, and Restore backup no longer worked after the new public Guest tour shipped. Source inspection found an explicit guard rejecting all restores whenever a demo was active; the file selector also hid its native input with display:none.
- **Behavior:** Validated backup selection leaves the tour untouched; the confirmation now clearly explains merging into real Guest data and exiting the demo. On confirm, a personal-ledger preflight and session guard run before removing isolated demo scope and restoring original file bytes and records through existing SHA/scoped merger. Existing Guest IDs win, demo records never merge. Invalid destination ledgers preserve the tour. Other development-only samples still block restore. Backup format and limits unchanged.
- **UX:** Use my Guest data is a compact, glass-like touch-safe pill with arrow icon, balanced against a lightweight Refresh demo control at 320px+. Restore backup has a focusable/touch-friendly native input and clear destination explanation.
- **Coverage:** New real-Settings unit regressions and Playwright Guest tour-to-restore and mobile control tests; final CI/visual approval recorded separately. No sample fixture/version change: no user-facing demo content/schema changed. No main or Firebase production change.

Busy state disables selectors, failures surface an error/toast, and unrelated Activity/finance/legacy Memory metadata is preserved. Diary untouched.

- GitHub CI 40550811870 passed 408/408 Node tests, lint, documentation and 108/109 Chromium tests. The only browser failure was a new Moments-page PDF regression expecting all `img` elements immediately, but `AttachmentGallery` intentionally creates photo elements only when their tiles enter the viewport via `IntersectionObserver`. - First CI after CHG-119 (run 40550500733) passed all 408/408 Node tests and documentation checks but lint stopped on the PDF filename sanitizer's explicit C0 control character range. Replaced that regex control range with the Unicode General Category `Cc` form accepted by the repository's strict `no-control-regex` rule, without weakening filename sanitization.

## CHG-20261027-119 | Download real PDF summaries from Dashboard/Moments and stabilize transaction-linked dropdown search
- Owner asked for genuine PDF instead of the Dashboard's plain-text `.txt`/Word-associated summary, the same download action from the Moments-page summary, image-only summary previews without visible filenames, and a reliable optional search experience when editing an existing transaction's Linked Moment dropdown. Work is confined to authorized `OldVersion` source. No new branch, main write, PR or Firebase deployment.
- Implemented a dependency-free, byte-accurate PDF-1.4 assembler and browser-canvas A4 report renderer. Both summary dialogs now expose Download PDF with busy/error feedback and use the same source: recorded Moment data, greeting, complete notes, Activities, descriptions, memories and available verified scoped original photos. Pages wrap long text, retain natural image proportions and are rendered as JPEG pages inside a real PDF with `application/pdf` MIME. PDF text is visual/raster (not selectable); this is a shareable report, not a full-originals backup. Missing originals/CORS-blocked legacy images are indicated as unavailable without inventing photo bytes. Download guards check the current account/Guest/Sample session before and after async original reads; no storage, schema or finance data mutations.
- Summary photo tiles omit visible image captions while retaining meaningful accessible names and existing click-to-preview/original-download controls. Non-image attachment labels still identify files. General attachment-gallery displays outside summaries remain unchanged.
- Transaction Form wraps custom select controls in non-label containers with explicit accessible labels so a click on a portaled search is not implicitly forwarded to a form label. Linked Moment/Activity dropdowns display choices first and offer Search only on explicit request when the list is long, rather than immediately revealing/focusing a text field. The shared dropdown keeps its previous default on every other view, and its iOS visual-viewport handling preserves an actively focused search when the keyboard changes the viewport. Changing a Moment retains the existing clearing of a stale Activity relationship.
- Added Node tests for PDF binary offsets/multipage output and name safety, plus Chromium checks for both download sources, no photo filenames in summaries, full story PDF page count, and a long-list edited transaction search/save preserving unrelated fields and linked-parent integrity. Updated the pre-existing Dashboard summary export test from text-file expectations to real PDF assertions.
- **Validation at authoring:** pending GitHub CI (Node/lint/Chromium/production build/documentation). Physical iOS Safari, real-account cloud, and live Hosting not independently verified. Owner retains local `npm run deploy:hosting` deployment control.

- Replaced every reachable legacy fixed filter panel and secondary Advanced toggle with the same embedded field/operator/value builder at each existing Filters location: Moments, global Activities, Moment Activities, global Transactions and Moment Transactions. Dynamic scoped record/recursive metadata/array discovery and optional schema/enum descriptors supply fields and actual distinct suggestions. Typed text, numeric range, calendar range/relative, boolean and set operators; nested AND/OR; removable logic-aware chips/counts; saved/recent/favorite presets; one-time legacy choice conversion; browser/account/Guest/Sample separation; debounced search/rules; and searchable keyboard/typeahead selectors with virtualized options are included. Finance totals compile one date-only matcher; unrestricted OR branches preserve all-date totals, while nondate conditions narrow history only.

No new business field, collection, ID, backup format, file-byte transfer, Rules or billing change. Previously lost cancellation cannot be inferred automatically. README/master/business guide were aligned; exactly five approved Markdown documents remain.

## CHG-20261026-100 | Activity cover positioning, list-order paging, completed-edit protection and full Moment summaries
- Owner requested completed Activities to require reopening before editing, completion dates at the end, a phone cover occupying 40% of the initial viewport with photo choice and drag positioning, clean Activity cards, detail swiping in Activities order, and complete Moment summaries including photos. Source and published version are 1.0.31, continuing the authorized Git/Firebase release workflow.
- Phone covers reserve 40svh; desktop retains bounded source-informed landscape geometry. The first supported Activity-linked image remains the default; Choose cover photo reuses the original-backed gallery for alternatives. Reposition enables pointer/touch drag or arrow keys with Done/Cancel. Percent offsets clamp within the scaled original's overflow; no image distortion, cropped derivative, byte duplication or new Activity field. Choice and per-image position use existing scoped persistent UI memory by Account/Guest, Sample, both IDs and image identity; these view preferences are local to this browser and excluded from backups. Original loading/integrity, unavailable-file treatment and URL cleanup remain shared.
- Completed/legacy-completed Activity field editors are hidden/blocked until Reopen, including cancelled records retaining completion. Explicit Cancel/Restore preserves legacy completion so it cannot bypass this rule; it never invents a completion date. List cards now contain only title/status/priority/parent/date and existing deliberate state/menu actions, with no description, gallery or expense preview. Detail keeps recorded Created then Completed dates last. Finance and original-file storage policies are unchanged.

Previous/Next controls and focus on the new heading provide a non-gesture path. No new collection or routing metadata in records/backups.
- Both Moment summaries show full notes, descriptions, memories and all photos/files without clamping or Show more. Shared original previews preserve full photo proportions; lazy images remain loaded for the open summary to stabilize scrolling and release on close. The shared dialog owns scrolling; only the topmost dialog handles keyboard focus/Escape. Dashboard summary text export includes notes, memories and filenames, without changing its text-file or backup format. Summary parent data is resolved from the active loaded scope.

## CHG-20261026-098 | Local attachment originals, read-only contextual lists and universal transaction creation
- Owner explicitly requested browser-local attachment originals with cloud filename metadata, no transaction editing in Activities/Moments, read-only Moment Activity cards, removal of Timeline, and publication to Git/Firebase. Follow-up clarified that contextual Add Transaction controls must also go because universal + already exists. Reviewed clean OldVersion base `3b30772637d4abbc40dde397e7519825c3dfca69`; fetched remote before writes and verified the identical local/remote tree. Main is untouched. Source version is 1.0.29.
- All new originals now save to the existing scoped IndexedDB repository for Account, Guest and Sample, with SHA-256 identity/integrity and the existing 10 MB file limit. Firebase stores the filename and required reference metadata (ID, hash, type/size/time and Activity link), never original bytes. Local-save failure creates no successful attachment reference. Guest sign-in transfer and backup restore copy originals into the destination browser scope, with no Storage upload. Reads first check local scope, allowing restored originals to work with retained legacy cloud markers; missing-local legacy cloud references retain read-only compatibility. No existing record/file is migrated or deleted. Device-only availability and backup transfer are stated in attachment areas/About/guide; metadata alone cannot recover missing bytes.
- Contextual transaction history in Activity Detail and Moments now uses read-only shared rows: normal selection/swipe cannot edit/delete, Details stays accessible, and Manage transactions links to main Finance. Removed contextual Add Transaction and existing-expense linking from Activity lists/detail/workspace. Universal + is the sole creation entry and presets the current Moment or both IDs/date on Activity Detail. Its guarded form preserves the route and failed draft, blocks repeated submissions/dismissal during save, rejects obsolete scopes and restores focus to the persistent + control. Main Finance retains edit/delete/link selection and its original source-routing/deduplication rules.
- Moment Activities uses read-only cards without menus, completion swipes/shortcuts or child/finance mutation callbacks; normal/keyboard selection still opens canonical Activity Detail, where Activity actions remain available. Removed Timeline UI/content. Five sections remain Overview, Activities, Transactions, Memories, Attachments; old Timeline URLs resolve to Overview as a non-writing compatibility alias. Shared menu semantics omit action disclosure attributes when a card has no actions. Existing Activity/Dashboard mutations elsewhere are retained.
- Main files: attachmentService and tests; ActivityAttachments; guestMergeService; ActivityList; TransactionHistory; LinkedTransactions; EventWorkspaceShell and handler fixtures; Activities and handler fixtures; DashboardLayout; momentWorkspaceRoutes/App; design-system CSS; ActivityDetail; About; package/lock; documentation checker, README/master/business guide and browser suites including new `e2e/local-attachments-readonly.spec.js`. Unreachable contextual mutation handlers/dialogs were retired. No Activity/finance collection, data migration, backup-v3 format change, Firebase billing/bucket/Rules or unrelated finance changes.
- **Executed:** `npm version 1.0.29 --no-git-tag-version`; `npm run lint` passed; `npm test` passed **335/335**, no failures/skips; `npm run test:e2e` passed **48/48**; `VITE_DEPLOY_ENV=Production npm run build` passed (2,504 modules, existing mixed Firebase import and >500 KB chunk warnings). `npm run check:docs -- 3b30772637d4abbc40dde397e7519825c3dfca69` and `git diff --check` passed; five approved Markdown documents/current links and all 97 prior audit entries preserved. Obsolete contextual transaction-creation tests were replaced with read-only wiring/navigation checks; equivalent creation/failure/duplicate/scope coverage moved to universal + browser journeys.

Browser journeys verify five reachable tabs, retired Timeline Overview, read-only cards/finance, retained main Finance editing, universal + presets/failed draft/retry/pending guard/focus/scope invalidation. Synthetic Account record stores with real IndexedDB verified local PNG preview, reload, metadata shape without bytes, backup/restore byte equality, distinct account isolation and unavailable-original fallback with **zero Firebase Storage requests**. Unit cases cover local failure, stale save results, legacy-marker local recovery, integrity, same-scope deduplication and no cloud writes. Guest/Sample regressions remain covered.

## CHG-20261025-095 | Activity Detail, Moment Transactions and deliberate Activity actions; prepare DN 1.0.28
- Owner requested the dedicated Activity page together with Moment grouping, contextual Transactions and simpler Activity cards. Reviewed clean OldVersion base `2474fab770951c9d84402d3449d0e70fc88a4717`; remote freshness matched before implementation and again during final review. Main is untouched. Package/lock and About prepare source **1.0.28**; no Hosting deployment was requested or executed. The latest verified published release remains 1.0.27 in CHG-094.
- Added `/events/:eventId/activities/:activityId`, resolving the exact scoped parent and nested child after authentication/data loading. The page reuses the Activity editor, safe rich-text renderer, transaction coordinator, gallery and original repository. It includes stable loading, focused retry/not-found states, archived context, in-place editing/state actions, finance and attachment workflows. Its single reserved landscape cover selects the first supported Activity-linked original/compatible HTTPS image in existing order; Moment-only photos are excluded, failed/metadata-only originals use a theme-aware fallback, and gallery preview/download remains available. Object URLs are released; no image bytes, crops, cover field, attachment hashes or stored descriptions are changed by opening the page.
- Added `/events/:eventId/transactions` and the six-section order Overview, Activities, Transactions, Timeline, Memories, Attachments. Overview now provides compact content summaries/links without loading attachment originals. The Transactions section reuses shared finance normalization/history/forms: typed ledger first, unified legacy second, nested expenses last, once per ID within its kind, preserving first values and known account sources. Deduplication occurs before Moment filtering. Full-Moment finite INR totals are independent of search/type/Activity/month filters; filters persist separately by scope/Sample/Moment and retain unavailable selections. The nested tier is rebuilt from live Activities so child deletion removes nested-only costs while retaining standalone finance. Add/edit/cancel/retry stay on the current route.
- Normal Activity selection across the overall list, Moment workspace and both Dashboard date views opens Activity Detail. Main navigation is Home (Dashboard), Activities, Moments, retaining existing labels/icons/Quick Create; the nested Activity route activates Activities and other Moment routes activate Moments. Cards no longer reserve a circular completion column. Deliberate left swipes Complete/Reopen, with distance/velocity and vertical-scroll safeguards; cancelled swipes only expose explicit menu actions, never Restore or Delete automatically. Transparent 44px three-dot triggers reuse portaled viewport-bounded menus, with keyboard state alternatives, Edit, contextual finance, Cancel/Restore and confirmed Delete last. Dashboard Ctrl/Command+Enter remains supported. Status updates use live announcements; failed edits/swipes retain recoverable drafts/records and block repeated submission.
- Activity and finance-mirror writers share a serialized scoped parent queue, resolve the latest child at execution, and preserve unrelated parent/Activity/nested-expense fields. Scope revisions guard late completion/rollback including A → B → A; Sample ownership guards loading and a missing fixture fails closed. Malformed/unreadable Guest Moment, income and expense reads propagate into focused loading errors instead of false missing/empty states or destructive overwrite attempts. Runtime scope/optimistic-create IDs remain store-only. Guest originals stay in scoped IndexedDB; Account references still require confirmed cloud originals, with no account-local fallback. No standalone Activity or Moment-finance collection, copied ledger, data migration or backup-format change was introduced. Existing multi-device/other whole-Moment concurrency and cross-collection compensation limits remain.
- Main additions: `src/features/activities/ActivityDetail.jsx`/CSS and cover/attachment components; `src/features/finance/components/LinkedTransactions.jsx`; `src/features/events/components/MomentContents.jsx`; shared scoped Activity/finance/original-loading hooks and `scopedRecordActions`/`activityDetail`/`momentTransactions` utilities; browser suites under `e2e/`; ESLint/Playwright configuration with an isolated Vite browser-test cache, and `scripts/check-documentation.mjs`. Main updates: App routing, DashboardLayout, Activities, EventWorkspaceShell, DashboardOverview, ActivityList, SwipeActionCard/SwipeToComplete, gallery/dialog/transaction-history/rich-text components, scoped stores/coordinator/event normalization, Guest event/income/expense readers, design tokens usage, package/lock, About, README, master and business guide. CI now includes lint, Chromium regressions and documentation checks alongside Node tests/build. DashboardActivityEditor is retained and reused.

Final `npm run lint` passed; `npm test` passed **336/336**, no failures/skips; `npm run test:e2e` passed **43/43** Chromium journeys; `npm run build` passed with **2,504 modules**, retaining the existing mixed Firebase config import and >500KB main-chunk warnings. `npm run check:docs -- 2474fab770951c9d84402d3449d0e70fc88a4717`, `node scripts/check-audit-log.mjs 2474fab770951c9d84402d3449d0e70fc88a4717` and `git diff --check` passed: five approved Markdown documents, valid current local links, all 94 earlier entries preserved, newest-first order. Added/updated regression coverage includes exact nested IDs/deep links/refresh/back-forward, loading/scope changes, A → B → A writes, missing Sample fixtures and malformed Guest reads/retry, swipe distance/velocity, finance precedence/provenance/finite totals, standalone retention, siblings/unknown fields/runtime metadata, safe notes, failed edits/transactions/swipes, duplicate requests, menu/focus/keyboard behavior and original loading/release/integrity. Browser validation exposed and corrected menu capture/queued-scroll dismissal, dialog focus restoration, native text-drag interference and a Dashboard flex-layout defect.
- **Executed responsive/browser evidence:** Chromium at 320×640, 360×740, 390×844, 844×390, 768×1024, 1024×768, 1280×480, 1440×900 and 1920×1080 in light/dark themes, plus reduced motion and 200% CSS zoom. Checks cover long titles/parent names/descriptions, six non-wrapping reachable links with active reveal, filter overflow, 44px menu targets, final detail content above the bottom dock, semantic headings/names, keyboard/menu/dialog focus and selected small-text contrast ≥4.5:1. ## CHG-20261023-088 | Compact everyday controls, quiet writing and retain original attachments
- Date: 2026-10-23. Owner requested smaller inline filters and transaction records, combined Dashboard date views, removal of Home Insights, quieter form writing, improved greetings and Activity photo previews. Reviewed base `89767c50581274c9c098984d83d4473c5e8a69e4` on OldVersion with remote matching that base; preserved the local CHG-086–087 work. No branch switch, commit, push or deployment; version remains 1.0.23.
- Filters retain the existing page and dropdown/sort logic in one horizontal row, approximately 52px high, with inline labels, sideways swiping, Reset and 44px arrow targets. Transactions use 13px title/amount and 11px metadata, with linked context and notes under native inline Details; sample rows measured approximately 88px collapsed. No saved transaction content was removed. Dashboard has separate Moments and Activities cards, each switching between Upcoming and actual same-calendar-day dates in prior years with a bounded 220px list. Removed View Insights from Home; Profile access remains. Moment history respects type visibility/archive/cancellation and does not treat every matching monthly day as an anniversary.
- All multiline fields now lazily load the existing visual Tiptap editor at every width. Removed permanent toolbars, Edit/Preview modes and word count. Nonempty text selection shows compact styles; Ctrl/Command+B/I, undo/redo and Alt+F10 support keyboards. Stored Markdown strings and untouched whitespace remain compatible; unsupported nesting/paste/required-text guards remain. Literal newly typed Markdown markers retain the existing grammar limitations. Greeting preview and escaped standalone HTML download share the same card design, with personalized text and restrained frame/typography.
- Originals up to 10 MB are addressed by SHA-256 and stored once per owner scope. Guest/sample files use isolated IndexedDB keys; account files use lazy Firebase Storage at `users/{uid}/attachments/{assetId}` and require confirmed upload before a record reference is saved. Parent attachment references can optionally link to an Activity; workspace and overall Activities show previews, while Attachments provides previews/removal and original download. Near-viewport loading and object-URL cleanup avoid fetching every offscreen file; viewer interactions do not open the underlying activity menu. Async saves recheck current owner, Moment, activity, route and mounted state, preserve the latest parent and guard duplicate submission. Removing a reference retains the original object; automatic orphan cleanup is not implemented.
- Compatible v3 JSON backups add optional deduplicated `attachmentFiles` byte payloads. Export/read/restore verify original hashes; validation rejects missing files, invalid sizes/base64/references and duplicate payloads. Both complete export and import are limited to 50 MB JSON; an oversized or unreadable backup fails instead of downloading a partial copy. Restore stores originals in the destination before record writes. Guest sign-in uploads originals before the Firestore batch and retains guest records/files on failure; owner/snapshot checks prevent subsequent stale work. Old metadata-only attachments cannot reconstruct originals and require reattachment. Existing IDs, stored note strings, guest/account boundaries and merge conflict policy remain.
- Executed: full `npm test` passed 284/284 with no failures or skips; final `npm run build` passed with 2485 modules. The existing main-chunk warning and mixed static/dynamic Firebase config import warning remain. Five-document/branding tests, diff whitespace and newest-first audit checks passed; all 85 committed historical entries remain unchanged. New real-handler/pure tests cover original-byte integrity, deduplication, scope isolation, denied uploads, corrupt/missing file rejection, activity preview parent identity, upload-before-record ordering, duplicate/retry guards, session changes, unmount/route changes and restore failures. Editor tests use real ProseMirror handlers; date/history and escaped greeting export tests pass. Mocks do not establish cloud security or account recovery.
- Executed local synthetic Guest browser checks: compact filters and transaction Details at phone/desktop widths; 372px phone and 1440px desktop layouts had no page overflow. Ctrl+B produced visible bold, undo returned plain text and selected text showed contextual styles without an ordinary-writing toolbar. Combined Dashboard views and removed Home Insights were inspected. File chooser → activity assignment → preview → reload/overall Activities → original download succeeded; downloaded bytes matched the input SHA-256. Backup JSON included one 68-byte original with matching hash/activity reference; same-backup Guest restore completed and the photo remained available. Greeting preview was inspected on phone/desktop. Development dependency-cache 504 errors were diagnosed and resolved by restarting Vite with forced optimization; no source workaround or dependency change was required.
- Source-derived: lazy Storage/account paths and safe failure ordering; no migration, Rules/configuration, package or dependency changes. Unverified: live bucket provisioning, Storage rules/CORS/cross-user isolation, signed-in file upload/read/backup/restore, interrupted cloud recovery, native phone keyboard/selection/gestures/PWA behavior and production publication. Whole-Moment concurrent-write, offline outbox and automatic backup limitations remain. Updated master, business guide and About.

## CHG-20261021-054 | Isolate income and expense caches on account switches
- Date (UTC): 2026-10-21.
- Actor/requester: DN owner; continued financial reliability work in the new chat.
- Branch / commit / PR: `OldVersion` only; pending commit (Git history will identify SHA). `main` untouched, PR #6 not merged.
- Scope and changed paths: `src/store/useIncomeStore.js`, `src/store/useExpenseStore.js`, `src/utils/financialLoadScope.js`, `src/utils/financialLoadScope.test.mjs`, `docs/MASTER_SOURCE_OF_TRUTH.md`, `docs/CHANGE_AUDIT_LOG.md`.
- Before -> after: Income cleared stale visible records on a scope change but retained old `loadedFor`; Expense did not clear old records at all. Both could incorrectly treat an old scope as cached after A → B → A navigation while requests overlapped; joining an in-flight request did not reactivate its scope. Both stores now clear scoped projections and `loadedFor` together on ownership change, check both keys before reusing cache, and reactivate pending loads before awaiting them.
- Why: Prevent cross-account/guest stale financial data from leaking into non-Financials screens or leaving an empty/stale transaction list after rapid session changes.
- User/data/security/compatibility impact: Client in-memory state only. Existing account Firestore paths, guest storage keys, transaction IDs and backup schema are unchanged; no data migration/deletion/deployment. This is defense in depth, not a substitute for server-side Firestore Security Rules.
- Verification (exact commands, PASS/FAIL/NOT RUN): Added six pure Node regression cases (`src/utils/financialLoadScope.test.mjs`); `npm test` and `npm run build` PENDING GitHub Actions; multi-account mobile browsers, overlapping live Firebase requests and Firestore Security Rules NOT RUN.
- Risk and rollback: Scope changes may temporarily show loading/empty lists until the selected account is fetched, intentionally avoiding previous-owner projection. Concurrent writes and live-account integration still need review. Revert this commit to roll back.
- Master sections updated: §9.1, §13, version 2.10.7.
- Status: SOURCE IMPLEMENTED / CI AND DEVICE QA PENDING.
- Follow-ups: Verify A → B → A switching with slow requests and account/guest/fixture modes; validate backup restore, expense edits and deployed rules.

- Date (UTC): 2026-10-21.

- Trigger: OldVersion CI run `39641812804`, application-smoke-tests failed with SyntaxError in `src/services/backupService.js` line 148; 40 tests passed, one test file failed to load, build was skipped.
- Actor/requester: AI fix in response to the owner's DN branding request.
- Branch/commit: `OldVersion`, atomic corrective commit (SHA in history); no `main` changes.
- Scope: `src/services/backupService.js`, `docs/MASTER_SOURCE_OF_TRUTH.md`, `docs/CHANGE_AUDIT_LOG.md`.
- Before -> after: A malformed string replacement accidentally inserted duplicate module contents into a v2-backup dashboard-key regex -> reconstructed parser from the last clean original source, with explicit DN/legacy prefixed setting lookups and correctly escaped dynamic regexes.
- Reason: Fix a real automated test failure and preserve old v1/v2 backup import compatibility.
- Impact: Restores existing backup parser compilation; no schema/version change or backend writes. New DN data remains canonical, older backups continue to import.
- Verification: CI failure inspected from job log and source rebuilt deterministically; rerun of `npm test`/`npm run build` via GitHub Actions expected after push, actual outcome to be inspected. Live Firebase/browser NOT RUN.
- Risk/rollback: Restore original pre-migration service via Git commit if needed; missing import or regex issues are covered by existing backup tests plus new DN v2 test.
- Master sections: §5, §7, §13; version 2.4.3.
- Status: SOURCE FIX COMMITTED, CI RERUN PENDING.

## CHG-20261021-009 | Switch guest data, settings and backup reads to DN keys
- Date (UTC): 2026-10-21.
- Requester: Repository owner (DN-only naming).
- Branch/commit: `OldVersion`; exact atomic commit in Git history; no `main` change.
- Changed paths: Auth/Guest Contexts, event/expense/income services, guest merge, Code List service, dev sample fixtures, Settings, Events, backup parser and backup tests, master and audit.
- Before -> after: Old prefixed app-owned browser keys -> `dn_` keys for active reads/writes; previously saved data copied by earlier migration before access. Legacy keys retained strictly for migration. Backup v1/v2 import accepts both generations. Successful account merge, guest exit and sample-data cleanup remove obsolete keys to prevent later reimport.
- Why: Finish compatibility-safe brand change across runtime state and prevent guest data resurrection.
- Data/security: No Firestore path/schema migration. Existing browser records never overwritten by old ones; explicit local-app-data deletion now removes both generations including dashboard favorites/recent. Original historic backup formats remain supported.
- Validation: Additional Node test created for DN-prefixed v2 backups; runtime `npm test`, `npm run build`, browser and live Firebase tests NOT RUN here.
- Risks/rollback: Compatibility logic needs real browser testing; older client versions may still expect old keys. Recover with Git history. Marked SOURCE MODIFIED, RUNTIME UNVERIFIED.
- Master: §§4.1, 4.2, 5, 13.
- Status: IMPLEMENTED IN SOURCE / VALIDATION PENDING.

## CHG-20261021-008 | Introduce non-destructive DN localStorage key migration
- Date (UTC): 2026-10-21.
- Actor/requester: Owner-directed DN brand rename.
- Branch / commit: `OldVersion`; atomic Git commit (SHA in repository history).
- Scope: `src/utils/storageMigration.js`, `src/utils/storageMigration.test.mjs`, `src/utils/codeLists.js`, `src/utils/codeLists.test.mjs`, `src/store/useAppStore.js`, `src/main.jsx`, master and audit.
- Before -> after: Preferences/Code Lists read or wrote old-prefixed browser keys -> copy old values into missing DN keys once (without deleting originals), read and write canonical DN preferences/Code Lists. Modern keys take precedence. Direct legacy code-list read also calls migration.
- Why: Change internal app branding without discarding existing browser data.
- Data/security/compatibility: Old data retained for downgrade, new canonical values never overwritten; guest/session and financial services will switch to new keys in the next commit. Only app-owned keys are eligible; modern keys win. No Firestore schema changes.
- Verification: Added four Node regression tests for copy/idempotency/clear/minimal-storage. Test execution and production build NOT RUN in this GitHub-connected environment; live Firebase NOT RUN. Source-reviewed.
- Risk/rollback: Partial migration may occur under storage quota denial, fails safely without deleting old keys. Rollback via Git commit; retain legacy values until verification.
- Master updated: §§4.1, 13.
- Status: SOURCE MODIFIED, RUNTIME UNVERIFIED.
